### [1] [Nonperturbative lattice simulation of the nucleation rate in thermal first-order phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18794)
*Jing Zhou,Ligong Bian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过非微扰格点方法研究了三维实标量场理论在有限温度下的真空衰变，结合扩展系综多重正则混合蒙特卡洛与实时间朗之万动力学，计算了气泡成核率，并外推至连续极限，发现与一阶微扰结果存在约8%偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限温度下真空衰变的非微扰机制，检验微扰理论在强耦合或临界区域的有效性。

Method: 采用扩展系综多-canonical混合蒙特卡洛采样临界气泡构型，结合实时间朗之万动力学模拟气泡后续演化；通过格点间距外推获得连续极限下的成核率。

Result: 得到 continuum-limit 下的真空衰变速率，与一环微扰预言相差约 8%。

Conclusion: 一环微扰论在该标量理论中虽定性可靠，但存在不可忽略的非微扰修正，凸显格点非微扰方法对真空衰变定量研究的必要性。

Abstract: We present a nonperturbative lattice study of vacuum decay through bubble nucleation in a three-dimensional real scalar field theory at finite temperature. The vacuum decay rate is computed by combining an expanded-ensemble multicanonical hybrid Monte Carlo approach, which samples the critical bubble configurations, with real-time Langevin dynamics, which follows the subsequent bubble evolution. We extrapolate the nucleation rate to the continuum limit and compare it with the corresponding one-loop perturbative prediction, finding a discrepancy of about \(8\%\).

</details>


### [2] [Fate of chiral and $U(1)_A$ symmetries in hot QCD matter under background magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19758)
*Dan Zhang,Heng-Tong Ding,José Javier Hernández Hernández*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD研究了外加磁场下手征对称性与单态U(1)_A对称性的破缺行为，发现弱场增强破缺、强场（尤其在近相变区）则抑制破缺，且对U(1)_A破缺的抑制较手征破缺更弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场（如重离子碰撞中产生的早期强场）对手征动力学和U(1)_A反常相关物理的影响，特别是对称性破缺模式如何随磁场演化。

Method: 采用固定尺度的(2+1)味HISQ格点QCD组态，轻夸克质量取m_l = m_s^phys/10（M_π ≈ 220 MeV），计算中性通道磁化率差：χ_π⁰ − χ_σ（表征手征破缺）与χ_π⁰ − χ_δ⁰（表征U(1)_A破缺），系统扫描不同磁场强度与温度依赖。

Result: 低温下，磁场增强两个磁化率差；在手征交叉温度附近，足够强的磁场反而抑制二者；抑制阈值磁场更强、且对χ_π⁰ − χ_δ⁰的抑制弱于χ_π⁰ − χ_σ。

Conclusion: 磁场对外加手征与U(1)_A对称性破缺具有双重调控作用——低T增强破缺，近T_c强场则削弱破缺，且U(1)_A破缺比手征破缺更鲁棒，暗示轴向反常效应在强场下仍具显著动力学权重。

Abstract: We summarize our recent lattice QCD study of chiral and singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking in background magnetic fields. The magnetic field explicitly reduces the non-singlet flavor symmetry through the unequal light-quark electric charges. We therefore study two neutral-channel susceptibility differences: $\chi_{\pi^0}-\chi_\sigma$ for chiral symmetry breaking and $\chi_{\pi^0}-\chi_{\delta^0}$ for singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking. The calculation uses fixed-scale $(2+1)$-flavor HISQ ensembles with $m_l=m_s^{\mathrm{phys}}/10$, corresponding to a zero-field pion mass of $M_\pi\simeq220\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Magnetic fields enhance both susceptibility differences at low temperatures. Near the crossover, sufficiently strong fields instead suppress both differences. The suppression sets in at larger magnetic fields and is milder for the singlet-axial susceptibility difference than for the neutral chiral susceptibility difference.

</details>


### [3] [Spatiotemporal K-Shape: Reconstructing inhomogeneous condensate profiles on the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19306)
*Keita Horie,Rin Kagami,Chiho Nonaka*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Spatiotemporal K-Shape的新方法，用于从格点蒙特卡洛系综中无模型假设地提取空间调制手征凝聚态的代表性实空间形状，并应用于(1+1)D Gross-Neveu模型，揭示了非正弦、多零点的非均匀序构型及其多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两体关联函数丢失傅里叶相位信息，无法确定非均匀序的真实空间构型；需发展能保留形状特征且不预设函数形式的分析工具，以刻画致密QCD有效模型中空间调制的手征凝聚态。

Method: 将K-Shape聚类算法推广至周期性格点场，支持平移对齐（及可选的整体符号/相位对齐），通过动态时间规整（DTW）度量形状相似性，提取聚类质心作为代表性构型；引入多重聚类比较评估单一质心是否足够表征系综。

Result: 在N_f=8的(1+1)D Gross-Neveu模型中，该方法成功提取出含两个符号翻转的非正弦调制轮廓，并在某一化学势下识别出多个形状家族共存；计入离散手征符号对称性对齐后，质心呈现不对称性，反映畴长涨落而非系综本征不对称。

Conclusion: Spatiotemporal K-Shape为非均匀量子相提供了互补于关联函数的实空间形状诊断工具，能无偏提取代表性构型并量化其多样性，是研究QCD相图中非均匀相的重要配置基础分析方法。

Abstract: Spatially modulated chiral condensates can affect the phase structure of dense QCD effective models. Understanding such order requires characterizing its real-space structure as well as detecting a modulation. Spatial two-point correlators retain the ensemble-averaged power spectrum but discard relative Fourier phases, leaving the configuration shapes undetermined in general. We propose the Spatiotemporal K-Shape method to extract representative shapes from lattice Monte Carlo ensembles without assuming a functional form. The method extends K-Shape clustering to periodic lattice fields, aligning configurations modulo translations and, optionally, a global sign or phase before extracting cluster centroids. Comparisons across clusterings assess whether one representative shape suffices. Controlled tests recover distinct profiles with identical correlators and separate mixtures of different shapes. Applied to the $(1+1)$-dimensional Gross-Neveu model with $N_f=8$, the method extracts non-sinusoidal profiles with two sign changes at two chemical potentials and separates multiple shape families at a third, where a single centroid is insufficient. Including the discrete chiral sign symmetry in the alignment can fold domain-length fluctuations into an asymmetric centroid without implying a preferred ensemble asymmetry. The method thus complements correlator diagnostics with representative real-space shapes and information on their diversity. The application to the Gross-Neveu model provides a testing ground for configuration-based studies of inhomogeneous order relevant to the QCD phase diagram.

</details>


### [4] [Global analysis of the $ρ$ resonance in lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21112)
*Luka Jevsenak,Luka Leskovec,Stefan Meinel,John Negele,Srijit Paul,Andrew Pochinsky*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过全局分析四个N_f=2+1味格点QCD组态，采用K矩阵参数化描述散射振幅，并将离散化效应仅置于格点能级而非振幅中，从而系统研究ρ共振态在物理极限下的极点位置及其对夸克质量与格距依赖性的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定ρ介子在物理夸克质量及连续极限下的复极点位置，以减小系统误差并厘清格点离散化与夸克质量外推所引入的模型依赖性。

Method: 采用K矩阵形式化处理s波ππ散射振幅，显式包含轻夸克与奇异夸克质量依赖；将格点离散化效应仅引入能级拟合函数，保持散射振幅定义于连续极限；对四套不同格距与夸克质量的N_f=2+1格点组态实施联合拟合，并通过变分参数化形式评估系统不确定性。

Result: 得到物理极限下ρ共振极点为√s_ρ = 744(11)(7) − i 69(7)(3) MeV，其中第一误差为统计误差，第二为系统模型依赖误差。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了传统振幅级外推中离散化与夸克质量依赖耦合导致的模型不确定性；模型平均结果表明ρ极点虚部（即衰变宽度）对参数化选择具有中等敏感性，而实部相对稳健。

Abstract: We present a lattice-QCD study of the $\rho$ resonance in which we address the quark-mass and lattice-spacing dependence in a novel way using a global analysis of four $N_f=2+1$ ensembles. We parametrize discretization effects in the lattice energy levels and not in the scattering amplitude, which is defined in the continuum. We describe the amplitude using a $K$-matrix parameterization, which contains the light- and strange-quark mass dependence. By varying the parameterizations of quark-mass and lattice-spacing dependence, we examine the sensitivity of the physical-limit $\rho$ pole position to these choices and obtain the result $\sqrt{s}_\rho = 744(11)(7) - i\,69(7)(3)~\text{MeV}$ from a model average.

</details>


### [5] [Canonical partition function and equation of state in the heavy quark region of finite-density lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20178)
*Shinji Ejiri,Hideya Hashizume,Masanari Koiida*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温密下格点规范理论的相结构与状态方程，聚焦于通过粒子数分解大正则配分函数所得到的正则配分函数；提出并首次在单味SU(2)规范理论中测试了一种适用于重夸克高密有效理论的正则配分函数计算算法，成功缓解符号问题（尤其在偶粒子扇区），并通过引入无穷小外场处理中心对称性导致的奇粒子扇区正则配分函数为零的问题；结果表明低温下物态方程符合强子共振气体模型，且未出现有限密度下的QCD物质软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限密度格点QCD中由夸克行列式复相位引发的严重符号问题，并厘清中心对称性对正则系综构造的约束及其在退禁闭相中的适用性。

Method: 基于粒子数分解的大正则配分函数构造正则配分函数；针对重夸克高密有效理论设计专用蒙特卡洛算法；在SU(2)规范理论中实施数值模拟；引入无穷小外部场破缺中心对称性以恢复奇粒子扇区非零贡献；由正则配分函数重构大正则量并提取热力学观测量。

Result: 确认符号问题在奇粒子扇区严重、偶粒子扇区较轻；揭示SU(N_c)中心对称性导致正则配分函数在N_c非整数倍粒子数时严格为零；通过外场方法实现全粒子数扇区的正则配分函数计算；重建的状态方程在低温下匹配强子共振气体行为；未发现有限密度下QCD物质软化迹象。

Conclusion: 粒子数分解结合外场调控是处理有限密度格点QCD符号问题与中心对称性约束的有效路径；重夸克极限下QCD热力学在宽温密范围内可由正则方法可靠描述，且不支持致密核物质或夸克物质的软化相变。

Abstract: We investigate the phase structure and equations of state in lattice gauge theory at finite temperature and finite density, focusing on the canonical partition function obtained by decomposing the grand partition function with respect to particle number. We demonstrate that when quark masses are heavy, the complex phase of the quark determinant, which causes the sign problem, can be controlled by decomposing the grand partition function according to particle number. In this study, we propose an algorithm to calculate the canonical partition function for heavy-quark, high-density effective theory and test it for the first time using one-flavor $SU(2)$ gauge theory, a case where the sign problem arises. When the grand partition function of $SU(2)$ is decomposed into canonical partition functions, the sign problem is serious in odd-particle sectors, while it is mild in even-particle sectors. The odd-particle sectors also expose a fundamental issue: center symmetry makes the canonical partition function of $SU(N_c)$ vanish unless the particle number is an integer multiple of $N_c$. Although appropriate in the confinement phase, this restriction must be treated carefully in the deconfinement phase. We resolve the issue by introducing an infinitesimal external field, calculate the canonical partition functions by Monte Carlo simulation, reconstruct the grand partition function, and determine thermodynamic observables over a wide range of temperature and density. Furthermore, we find that the chemical-potential dependence of the equation of state at low temperatures exhibits behavior characteristic of the hadron resonance gas model, and that no softening of the QCD matter occurs at finite density.

</details>


### [6] [Transferable neural-network preconditioners for Wilson--Clover propagator inversions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20990)
*Shiyi Zhong,Yuanyi Zhang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Dian-Jun Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出两种单层神经网络架构，用于加速四维Wilson-Clover传播子求逆，均支持跨不同格点体积、格距和π介子质量的零样本迁移，且可组合使用以进一步减少GCR迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速格点QCD中Wilson-Clover费米子传播子的数值求逆，降低计算成本，尤其针对大体积格点系综。

Method: 1) Gauge-covariant parallel transport convolution (PTC) 网络，直接减少广义共轭残差（GCR）迭代次数；2) 类CNN网络结合多重网格启发式算法；二者可联合使用。所有方法均实现零样本迁移（no retraining）。

Result: PTC网络在不同格点体积、格距和π质量下保持稳定加速；类CNN网络从小体积到大体积格点系综可直接迁移并保持强加速；组合方案进一步降低GCR迭代；整体方案在大体积系综中相较小体积更具计算优势。

Conclusion: 所提神经网络方案为格点QCD传播子求逆提供了高效、可迁移、可组合的加速范式，特别适用于大规模物理量计算。

Abstract: We investigate two single--layer neural-network architectures for accelerating four-dimensional Wilson--Clover propagator inversions on lattice ensembles in practical use; both transfer directly without retraining. The first is a gauge-covariant parallel transport convolution (PTC) network that directly reduces the number of generalized conjugate residual (GCR) solver iterations. The second is a CNN-like network used together with a multigrid-inspired algorithm. The two approaches can be combined to further reduce GCR iterations. PTC transfers without retraining across lattice volumes, lattice spacings, and pion masses while maintaining comparable acceleration, whereas the CNN-like network transfers directly from small- to large-volume ensembles with strong acceleration. A cost comparison accounting for both basis construction and solves shows that the proposed scheme is more advantageous for large-volume ensembles than for small-volume ones.

</details>


### [7] [Finite temperature Sp(4) lattice gauge theory with matter fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21493)
*Chris Allton,Ed Bennett,Luigi Del Debbio,Ryan C. Hill,Deog Ki Hong,Jong-Wan Lee,C.-J. David Lin,Biagio Lucini,Maurizio Piai,Davide Vadacchino,Alexis Verney-Provatas*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在有限温度下对Sp(4)规范理论（耦合两个基本表示下的Wilson-Dirac费米子）开展格点研究，旨在确定其热相变阶数（尤其是否为一级相变），以评估其在SIMP暗物质模型中产生可探测引力波背景的潜力。通过Polyakov圈及其磁化率、重加权与有限体积分析，发现中等轻费米子质量下为平滑交叉，而最大费米子质量下首次观测到一级相变迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sp(4)规范理论在复合动力学扩展标准模型中具有关键地位，尤其作为强相互作用大质量粒子（SIMP）暗物质候选者；若存在一级热相变，将在早期宇宙中产生可观测的随机引力波背景，需定量确定相变强度以评估未来实验灵敏度。

Method: 在格点上系统扫描耦合常数、时间维尺度（N_t=4,6）、空间体积及费米子质量参数空间；测量Polyakov圈及其磁化率；应用多直方图重加权技术；执行有限体积标度分析；以纯Sp(4)杨-米尔斯理论为基准验证算法与分析流程。

Result: 纯Sp(4)规范场确认存在一级相变；引入中等轻费米子后，Polyakov圈显示平滑交叉行为，无临界奇点；在当前可达的最大费米子质量下，首次获得一级热相变的明确格点证据。

Conclusion: Sp(4)规范理论在重费米子极限下存在一级热相变，支持其作为SIMP暗物质模型的可行实现；该结果亟需更大规模格点模拟以在连续极限下精确定征相变性质，并推广至更一般的Sp(2N)规范群理论。

Abstract: We perform the first finite-temperature lattice study of the Sp(4) gauge theory coupled to two Wilson-Dirac fermions transforming in the fundamental representation. Its continuum limit plays an important role in many extensions of the Standard Model for particle physics with composite dynamics. For example, it provides a compelling realisation of the strongly interacting massive particle (SIMP) paradigm for dark matter. If realised in nature, it might lead to a first-order thermal phase transition in the early universe, and produce a detectable relic stochastic background of gravitational waves (GW). Assessing whether future experiments are sensitive to the resulting GW power spectrum requires determining the strength of the transition. We scan the parameter space of this lattice theory with a fine mesh of choices of lattice coupling, for two small values of the temporal extent of the lattice, and several choices of spatial volume and fermion mass. We measure the Polyakov loop and its susceptibility on the ensembles generated, implement reweighting, and perform a finite volume analysis, both to classify the nature of the phase transition, and to estimate the critical coupling. We validate our algorithms and analysis procedures on the better understood Sp(4) Yang-Mills theory, by confirming the presence of a first-order phase transition. Upon coupling the theory to matter fields, in the presence of moderately light fermions, the Polyakov loop shows a clear crossover behaviour and does not detect singularities associated with a phase transition. We also uncover the first evidence of a first-order thermal phase transition in the case of the largest fermion masses available. This finding motivates future, large-scale studies aimed at characterising the transition in the continuum limit, for this and closely related theories with Sp(2N) gauge group.

</details>


### [8] [Hadron structure on the 16-cell honeycomb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21543)
*Sandor Katz,Daniel Nogradi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在16-cell蜂巢格点上非微扰QCD的计算，相较于传统立方格点，该格点具有更大的时空对称性，从而减小截断效应、抑制算符混合与幂次发散，并提升对强子结构函数（包括极化/非极化、正向/非正向、扭度-2/扭度-3矩阵元）格点计算的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统立方格点上因对称性不足导致的较大截断效应、算符混合及幂次发散问题，提升强子结构函数的格点QCD确定精度。

Method: 利用16-cell蜂巢格点更高的空间群对称性（对应更大的不可约表示集），系统分析扭度-2与扭度-3算符在极化/非极化、正向/非正向矩阵元中的对称性约束、混合模式与重整化行为。

Result: 证明在16-cell格点上：(i) 更多扭度矩可实现类连续谱重整化；(ii) 静止强子可承载更多不可约表示，降低计算复杂度；(iii) 显著抑制幂次发散与算符混合。

Conclusion: 16-cell蜂巢格点为高精度格点计算强子结构函数（尤其是高阶矩与非正向矩阵元）提供了更优离散化方案，其增强的对称性直接带来物理可观测量的更优连续极限外推性质。

Abstract: In previous work we started a program of studying non-perturbative QCD on the 4-dimensional 16-cell honeycomb lattice instead of the usually adopted cubic lattice. It was shown that in several observables much smaller cut-off effects are observed, undoubtedly a result of the larger space-time symmetry of this discretization. In the present work further consequences are worked out, relevant for the lattice determination of hadronic structure functions. The key ingredient is the larger set of irreducible representations of the 16-cell honeycomb symmetry group relative to the cubic lattice, leading to more protection from mixing and thus less power divergences. A second ingredient is that more irreducible representations are available for a hadron at rest, which also leads to computational gain in practice. The analysis is given in full detail for both polarized and unpolarized and both forward and off-forward matrix elements of twist-2 and twist-3 operators. It is shown that a larger set of moments can be determined with continuum-like renormalization and at rest, than on the cubic lattice.

</details>

### [9] [Sensitivity of the SHiP experiment at CERN to sterile-neutrino-induced oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18824)
*SHiP Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SHiP实验利用双基线探测器配置，通过中微子振荡探测轻质量惰性中微子，在6×10²⁰质子打靶下，对μ与e通道的|U_μ4|²和|U_e4|²给出95%置信度灵敏度限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测轻质量（~eV量级）惰性中微子，检验短基线中微子异常（如LSND、MiniBooNE）所暗示的3+1振荡模型。

Abstract: This paper reports a high-statistics study of antiproton--argon ($\bar{p}\text{--}\text{Ar}$) annihilation at rest in the J-PARC T98 experiment using a single-phase Liquid Argon Time Projection Chamber (LArTPC). A dataset comprising over $10^{5}$ annihilation events was acquired together with comprehensive calibration data. In parallel, a dedicated Geant4-based simulation framework was developed to model the secondary beam transport and detector response. Together, these experimental and computational resources provide a foundation for benchmarking hadronic interaction models and for future LArTPC-based antimatter searches, such as GRAMS. As an initial application of this framework, we compare the measured annihilation topologies, localized energy profiles, and spatial cluster multiplicities with predictions from three hadronic interaction generators: Fritiof, INCL++, and GiBUU. While all models reproduce the overall features of the total energy deposition, GiBUU provides the most consistent empirical description of localized energy deposition near the annihilation vertex and spatial cluster multiplicities at larger radii, reflecting the key roles of secondary nucleon knockout and in-medium pion absorption. These results provide a high-statistics experimental benchmark for modeling low-energy antiproton interactions in liquid argon.

The branching-fraction ratio $\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} \gamma]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{+} \gamma]$ is measured to be $0.74 \pm 0.05 ({\rm stat.}) \pm 0.06 ({\rm syst.)}$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 文献综述法，按理论解释框架、探测技术分类、近年实验进展、未来实验布局、关键科学挑战五个维度进行结构化分析，强调多技术路线的互补性。

Result: 确认当前最灵敏实验（如XENONnT、PandaX-4T、LZ）在GeV–TeV质量区间仍无可靠信号；亚GeV暗物质探测取得进展（如SENSEI、SuperCDMS）；年调制（DAMA/LIBRA）与方向探测（DRIFT、CYGNUS）仍存争议；中微子地板已成为高灵敏度实验的主要极限。

Conclusion: 多技术路线协同探测是突破暗物质参数空间的关键；下一代实验必须在极低阈值、本底建模与鉴别、中微子背景应对三方面取得实质性进步，方能实现决定性发现或对主流模型施加终极约束。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter remains one of the major open questions in particle physics and cosmology. Direct detection experiments aim to identify dark matter particles through their interactions with ordinary matter, but despite substantial improvements in experimental sensitivity, no conclusive detection has been achieved. In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview of the present status and future prospects of direct particle detection, focusing on searches for dark matter particles from the Galactic halo. We introduce the theoretical framework for interpreting experimental results, including nuclear and electron scattering, collective excitations, particle absorption, and time- and direction-dependent signatures. We review the detection technologies employed in these searches, highlighting their operating principles, advantages, and limitations, as well as the calibration techniques and background mitigation strategies required to achieve high sensitivity. Particular emphasis is placed on experimental results reported since 2020, covering nuclear and electron recoil searches, annual modulation measurements, and directional detection. We also discuss experiments under construction and proposed future detectors, ranging from large-scale noble-liquid experiments to low-threshold technologies targeting sub-GeV dark matter. Finally, we examine the challenges that will shape the next generation of experiments, including the need for increasingly low energy thresholds, improved background understanding and discrimination, and the growing importance of neutrino-induced backgrounds. The diversity and complementarity of detector technologies will remain essential for exploring the dark matter parameter space and establishing the nature of any potential signal.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM驱动的科学计算提供了首个可行范式：当配备适当工具链（MCP）、领域知识注入（custom resources）与轻量量化模型时，文本提示可有效触发物理仿真流程；但复杂模拟仍需更完备的示例库与结构化领域知识支撑。

Abstract: Recent LLM developments have seen AI-generated code become commonplace in the software development industry. Without tool access, LLMs cannot execute simulations, and their knowledge of toolkits such as Geant4 is limited. We demonstrate here, a detector simulation workflow with a Qwen3.6-27B model with Q4_K_M GGUF quantisation together with custom resources and tools served via MCP servers to generate a particle detector simulation. In 50 independent runs, 42(29--56)% at 95% CI produced energy-deposit distributions statistically consistent with a reference simulation, measured against a reference simulation. These results provide a proof of concept that MC simulations can be generated using text-based prompts with an LLM, given the necessary resources and tools. More extensive resources, such as example code and additional documentation, are likely to be required for more complex simulation tasks.

Abstract: The study of jet substructure provides tests of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) and offers differential ways to study hadronization mechanisms. QCD predicts that jet radiation patterns depend on the mass and color charge of the initiating parton. First, we present the first measurement of the Energy-Energy Correlator (EEC) for $\textrm{D}^0$-meson-tagged jets. This observable reveals the detailed structure of QCD radiation from heavy quarks, allowing for a distinction between perturbative and non-perturbative effects in charm-quark showers. We then discuss measurements of the angular differences between three different axis definitions inside the jets. The three definitions offer different sensitivity to wide-angle soft radiation and systematically probe the radiation pattern from charm quarks. The final measurement regards jet angularities, which characterize the weighted angular distribution of the jet constituents. These powerful observables probe the parton shower, with sensitivity to parton-mass and Casimir-factor related effects that can be tuned by adjusting the importance of wide-angle radiation.

Specifically, it has been shown that a supersymmetric 4D GUT based on SU(5), with finitely many chiral multiplets, cannot be broken spontaneously to the exact minimal supersymmetric standard model (MSSM) while preserving a discrete $\mathbb{Z}_M^R$, with $M \geq 3$, or continuous U(1) $R$-symmetry. Abstract: We present the first systematic analytical calculation of the local and nonlocal cosmological collider (CC) signals generated by massive fermion bubble loops in the presence of an effective chemical potential during inflation. Working in the de Sitter approximation, we investigate the four-point correlator of inflaton perturbations in the single-squeezed limit. By combining CC cutting rules, late-time expansions, and partial Mellin-Barnes (MB) techniques, we derive analytical expressions for both local and nonlocal signals. In particular, we identify two distinct contributions to the local signal, arising from spectrum and integer poles and from ultraviolet poles of the loop-momentum integral. By reorganizing the MB representation, we demonstrate that these singularities cancel in the complete signal and obtain a convergent double-MB integral suitable for numerical evaluation. Independent calculations using complementary MB representations provide nontrivial consistency checks of our results. We find that the chemical potential can significantly enhance the oscillatory signal while increasing its logarithmic oscillation frequency. Our analytical results provide a foundation for further studies of chemical-potential-enhanced fermionic CC signals, including fermion triangle loops.

Indeed, since the U($1$) inside the U($N$) YM-like theories decouples, the large-$N$ U($N$) and SU($N$) expansions should either essentially coincide or intrinsically differ - in the latter case necessarily by extra - possibly singular - topologies, as the 't Hooft expansion in U($N$) YM-like theories already exhausts all the smooth topologies. Abstract: The fuzzy-sphere regularization provides a powerful framework for studying three-dimensional (3D) conformal field theories (CFTs) through radial quantization, but conformal defects have so far been studied with it only by exact diagonalization (ED) of small systems. Abstract: Does the special-conformal connection remain an auxiliary field when a spontaneously broken conformal gauge theory is promoted to a fully dynamical Yang-Mills-Higgs theory, and under what conditions does its elimination yield Weyl gravity? The additional gauge and scalar sectors can modify its algebraic solution, so the exact Weyl reduction is restricted to the source-free branch. We further investigate the linearised spectrum of the reduced Weyl theory around de Sitter spacetime, identifying massless and partially massless spin-2 branches, with the latter occurring at $m_{\mathrm{PM}}^2=2\Lambda/3$. Finally, we discuss the subsequent symmetry breaking induced by an antisymmetric scalar $6$-plet and its role in connecting the Weyl-gravity phase to Einstein gravity.

Instead, the unit of charge enters the rim through a simple pole: the extremal eigenvalue of the rim operator passes through infinity in the same flux interval as the charge jump, with $\Delta Q_{\rm edge}=\tfrac12\Delta\,\mathrm{sign}\,\lambda_{\rm ext}$. The residue factorizes into the coupling of the inner edge mode to the rim and the inverse slope of that mode's level, and grows linearly with the radius. All statements are checked numerically on disks of radius $R=11$, $19$ and $34$.

Abstract: We construct homogeneous anisotropic black branes in Einstein gravity and Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton theory in arbitrary spacetime dimensions. Abstract: Metric solution spaces defined by gauge and boundary conditions are associated with a residual symmetry algebra, consisting of the diffeomorphism generators whose action preserves these conditions. Describing a metric solution space in terms of tetrad variables adds a local Lorentz freedom. When part of this freedom is fixed by additional tetrad gauge and boundary conditions, metric residual symmetries must be supplemented by compensating Lorentz transformations. If a residual Lorentz freedom remains, there is an ambiguity in these compensating transformations. The most direct choices generally fail to realize the metric residual symmetry algebra as a subalgebra of the tetrad one, because of a field-dependent cocycle. Abstract: Instant Folded Strings (IFSs) present a puzzle. On the one hand, they appear to be an intrinsic part of string theory: they are fundamental closed strings nucleated classically in backgrounds in which the string coupling grows toward the future, and they have been argued to play an important role in black-hole interiors and cosmology. On the other hand, their energy-momentum tensor violates the Averaged Null Energy Condition (ANEC), placing IFSs in tension with standard causality and unitarity arguments. The complete state has vanishing total energy and momentum while remaining highly excited and carrying negative integrated pressure on every finite time slice. TL;DR: 本文构建了正水平Wess-Zumino-Witten（WZW）模型的神经实现，结合玻色Wakimoto与费米Fourier-Berezin场；利用等级-秩对偶揭示费米构造中带电扇区的神经可观测量可复现共形块数据，并支持对偶理论间未知关联函数的信息迁移；同时指出同一连续流代数的不同神经表征呈现差异化的有限宽度行为，其逼近精度依赖于网络架构、正则化方案与参数分布；最终建立了共形场论精确数学结构与神经场论表达力及计算性质之间的联系。

Motivation: 将共形场论（CFT）的严格代数结构（如当前代数、共形块、等级-秩对偶）嵌入可学习的神经场论框架，以理解深度学习模型在物理先验约束下的表达能力与泛化机制。

Conclusion: 神经场论并非仅是CFT的经验拟合工具，而是能承载并揭示其深层对称结构（如对偶性、扇区分解）的可微分表示框架；有限宽度效应构成连接代数结构与学习动力学的新桥梁，为构建具备物理一致性的AI for Science模型提供理论基础。

Abstract: We develop neural realizations of positive-level Wess-Zumino-Witten models using bosonic Wakimoto and fermionic Fourier-Berezin fields. Level-rank duality connects the charged sectors of the fermionic construction, where explicit neural observables recover conformal block data and enable the transfer of information about unknown correlators between paired theories. We also show that distinct neural presentations of the same continuum current algebra exhibit different finite-width behavior, with approximation accuracy depending on architecture, regulator, and parameter distribution. These results connect the exact mathematical structure of conformal field theory with the expressive and computational properties of neural field theories.

</details>

Abstract: We study a surprisingly efficient family of variational ansatze for the ground state of bosonic matrix quantum mechanics at large $N$. These are ansatze whose norm-squared probability distributions are themselves matrix integrals with single-trace actions, and so have their own independent `t Hooft expansions. They are efficient in the sense that optimizing over a small number of parameters produces many digits of accuracy for the energy, single-trace moments, and connected correlators. For infinite-$N$ planar data our results rapidly converge to within published bootstrap intervals and stabilize to a few digits of precision beyond them. We also demonstrate that we can efficiently capture $1/N$ corrections by computing connected two-point functions. These are matched against resummed quantum perturbation theory and Monte Carlo simulations.

Abstract: We construct Baxter $Q$-operators for a family of quantum cluster integrable systems, that we call the flavored Toda chains, associated with quivers $\mathcal{Q}_N[F]$, where $F\subseteq\{2,\dots,N\}$ specifies data of $\vert F \vert$ additional nodes compared to $\mathcal{Q}_N[\varnothing]$ for the ordinary $N$-particle open $q$-deformed Toda chain. A distinguished sequence of quantum cluster mutations yields an ordered product of Faddeev's quantum dilogarithms. We prove that these operators form a commuting family and that their shifted quotients are transfer matrices whose coefficients are commuting Hamiltonians. We identify these transfer matrices with matrix elements of products of explicit local Lax matrices of rank two. Local cluster transformations move flavor insertions along the chain while preserving their ordered flavor parameters and the transfer matrix together with the canonical transformation. We also derive integral kernels for the $Q$-operators and evaluate an associated Fourier transform as a factorized scalar $Q$-function. Finally, we relate the quivers to the BPS quivers of five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories in the decoupling limit and identify the normalized scalar $Q$-function with the perturbative contribution of a canonical codimension-two defect wrapping a squashed three-sphere.

Abstract: We construct oscillator realisations of Yangian Lax operators in type $A$, with particular interest in non-factorisable operators associated with prime snake modules. Our construction applies to finite-dimensional skew representations of $Y(\mathfrak{gl}_n)$. We prove that these representations are generated by the tensor vacuum in suitably ordered tensor products of oscillator representations. Their Lax operators are obtained by restricting the corresponding products of linear oscillator Lax operators to these submodules. We determine the vacuum eigenvalues and Drinfeld polynomials, and give a determinant formula for the dimensions directly in terms of the highest loop weights. A $21$-dimensional example illustrates the construction, including an explicit basis and a transfer-matrix identity.

Abstract: We investigate the interplay of observables and symmetries in the framework of causal fermion systems (CFS). We show that position observables transform covariantly under unitary symmetries and for static CFS we derive their Heisenberg-type evolution. To give these results a geometric interpretation, we establish conditions under which spacetime isometries induce symmetries of CFS constructed from solutions of the Dirac equation. The crucial link is provided by the Dirac equation itself, which relates the infinitesimal generators of the geometric and CFS symmetries. In static spacetimes, this allows us to identify the generator of the induced unitary symmetry with the spatial Dirac Hamiltonian.

</details>

Abstract: The Bagci-Hoggan exponential-type orbitals, the generalization of Coulomb-Sturmians to noninteger quantum numbers, were recently identified as d-dimensional Coulomb-Sturmians. In the present study, this identification is treated in detail. The radial differential equation for these orbitals is derived directly from their closed form. It represents the general Coulomb-Sturmian differential equation for quantum numbers with real values. The solution is verified through a power series representation and a first-order matrix form based on the Green's function of the radial Laplacian. New transformation relationships between these orbitals and Slater-type orbitals with noninteger principal quantum numbers are established. The radial differential equation is expressed in terms of the radial momentum operator. The expectation values of the position and radial momentum operators are obtained in closed form. The radial and Heisenberg uncertainty products, the Shannon entropy and the Fisher information are derived in general form.

</details>

Abstract: We study Einstein's nonsymmetric metric-affine Lagrangian density with a generally nonsymmetric metric and an affine connection treated as independent variational variables. The resulting connection equation belongs to the classical Einstein--Hlavatý--Tonnelat framework, while the unrestricted metric Euler--Lagrange equation is analyzed without a divergence constraint on the antisymmetric metric density. Its symmetric part takes the form of Einstein-type gravitational equations with geometrically induced source terms and reduces to the vacuum Einstein equations in the torsion-free limit. The antisymmetric part becomes a geometric constitutive relation based on the irreducible decomposition of torsion. Its contracted four-divergence yields a general vector-field equation with geometric source and response terms. Under a trace--axial reduction, $Q=0$ gives a source-free Maxwell-type equation, whereas constant nonzero $Q$ gives a Proca-type equation. Independently, a trace of the generalized Bianchi identities yields the homogeneous Maxwell-type pair associated with the torsion trace, while the connection equation implies a Lorenz-type condition. Thus gravitational, constitutive, Maxwell--Proca-type, and Lorenz-type structures arise as compatible consequences of one metric-affine variational system. The antisymmetric dynamical level is generated by the contracted four-divergence of its constitutive level. No independent electromagnetic or matter field is introduced.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Hooke-Newton对偶性及其在经典与量子力学中任意幂律势下的推广；进而研究Wigner-Yang量子力学（基于Dunkl算子的Heisenberg-Schrödinger量子力学变形）中谐振子与类库仑势的本征值问题，发现该对偶性仍成立，并可借此映射求解；进一步将此推广至任意幂律势，并指出Wigner-Yang体系中需对形变参数作类似Langer修正的反射算子依赖型重标度，而能量-耦合常数交换关系在经典、标准量子及Wigner-Yang三者中保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Hooke-Newton对偶性至非标准量子框架（Wigner-Yang量子力学），探究其在Dunkl型变形量子体系中的适用性与结构性约束，特别是对偶性在本征值问题求解与参数重标度中的普适性。

Method: 运用Dunkl微分-差分算子形式化Wigner-Yang量子力学；构造谐振子与类库仑势的本征方程；通过幂律势下广义缩放变换与反射算子作用下的形变参数重标度，建立Hooke-Newton型对偶映射；对比经典、标准量子及Wigner-Yang三类体系中的Langer型修正机制与能量-耦合常数关系。

Result: 证实Hooke-Newton对偶性在Wigner-Yang量子力学中依然成立，可用于解析谐振子与类库仑势的本征谱；导出适用于任意幂律势的广义对偶映射；揭示Wigner-Yang体系中形变参数需经含反射算子的Langer类重标度，而能量-耦合常数交换关系具有跨范式不变性。

Conclusion: Hooke-Newton对偶性是比标准量子力学更深层的结构对称性，在Wigner-Yang量子力学中得以保持并自然诱导出反射算子参与的形变参数重标度机制；该对偶性统一刻画了经典、标准量子与Dunkl型变形量子三类体系中幂律势物理的共性缩放行为与能谱对称性。

Abstract: In this work we first revisit the Hooke-Newton duality and its generalisation to arbitrary power laws in classical and quantum mechanics. We then consider Wigner-Yang quantum mechanics on the real line, which is a deformation of standard Heisenberg-Schrödinger quantum mechanics closely related to Dunkl's differential-difference operator formalism. We consider the corresponding eigenvalue problem for the harmonic potential and a Coulomb-like potential. We observe that the Hooke-Newton duality is also applicable to these deformed quantum systems allowing us to solve the eigenvalue problem of one by mapping it onto the other system. This approach is then generalised to arbitrary power-law potentials in Wigner-Yang quantum mechanics. Whereas in standard quantum mechanics the classical re-scaling of angular momenta has to be applied to Langer-modified quantum angular momenta, Wigner-Yang quantum mechanics requires a rescaling of deformation parameters similar to the Langer-type modification but involving the reflection operator. The energy coupling-constant exchange, however, remains the same in all three cases, classical mechanics, quantum mechanics and Wigner-Yang quantum mechanics.

</details>
