  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定氦负离子是否存在稳定的三电子束缚态，这是量子多体问题中关于电子关联与束缚能的基本问题。

Method: 采用非相对论库仑模型，假设原子核质量无穷大、电子自旋为1/2；通过将哈密顿量相对于中性氦基态作分解，并完成平方（completion of the square），将问题约化为一个一维变分不等式。

Result: 严格证明E^F(3,2) = E^F(2,2)，即三电子体系（He⁻）的基态能量等于两电子体系（He）的基态能量，故不存在束缚的第三个电子；氦负离子无三电子基态。

Conclusion: 氦负离子在该理想化非相对论模型中不具稳定性，不形成束缚态；该结论由严格的变分分析与能量比较得出。

Abstract: We study the helium anion in the nonrelativistic Coulomb model with an infinitely massive nucleus and spin-$\frac12$ electrons. We prove the absence of binding, with $E^{\mathrm{F}}(3,2)=E^{\mathrm{F}}(2,2)$ and no three-electron ground state. A decomposition relative to the helium ground state and completion of the square reduce the problem to a one-dimensional variational inequality.

</details>


### [102] [Integrability of the Schrödinger Equation for a Mie-type Potential via the NU Method and Appell-type Hypergeometric Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19622)
*Kikunga Kasenda Ivan,Omalohoso Tasa Joshua,Likwolo Katamba Stanislas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Appell型超几何多项式与Mie型势径向薛定谔方程精确解之间的形式对应，利用Nikiforov-Uvarov方法导出含有效主量子数N的超几何微分方程；通过渐近分析揭示其双重结构：第二类合流超几何函数U给出平方可积束缚态（N∈ℕ为Diophantine型量子化条件），广义拉盖尔函数L给出非归一化散射态；在l=0情形下，后者截断为Appell型多项式ẽₙ，从而刻画Mie型势的s波散射。工作统一了束缚态与散射态的代数描述，并明确了Appell型多项式的物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明Appell型超几何多项式在量子力学中的物理含义，特别是其与Mie型势下径向薛定谔方程解的对应关系，并统一处理束缚态与散射态的代数结构。

Method: 采用Nikiforov-Uvarov (NU) 方法将径向Schrödinger方程约化为含参数N的超几何型微分方程；结合渐近分析区分第二类合流超几何函数U与特定类型广义拉盖尔函数L的物理角色；考察l=0极限下的多项式截断行为。

Result: 发现N∈ℕ构成Diophantine型量子化条件，导出离散束缚态能谱；U函数对应束缚态，L函数对应散射态；l=0时L函数退化为Appell型多项式ẽₙ，精确刻画s波散射振幅。

Conclusion: Appell型多项式ẽₙ本质上是Mie型势在零角动量下散射态解的多项式截断形式，该工作构建了束缚态与散射态共用的统一超几何代数框架，拓展了特殊函数在可解势模型中的物理诠释。

Abstract: We establish a formal correspondance between the Appell-type hypergeometric polynomials $\widetilde{e}_n$, rectently introduced in [1], and the exact solutions od the radial Schrödinger equation for a family of Mie-type potentials. Using the Nikiforov-Uvarov (NU) method, we show that the radial equation reduces to an hypergeometric differential equation with a parameter $N$, which plays the role of an effective principal quantum number. A careful analysis of th asymptotic behavior of the solutions reveals a dual structure: the confluent hypergeometric function of the second kind $U$ yields square-integrable bound states, for which the condition $N\in \mathbb{N}$ provides a quantization condition of Diophantine type, and a discrete energy spectrum. In contrast, the generalized Laguerre function $L$, of a certain type, yields non-normalizable scattering states. In the particular case of vanishing angular momentum $(l=0)$, these scattering stetes truncte into the Appell-type polynomial $\widetilde{e}_n$, which thus characterize the s-wave scattering of the Mie-type potential. This work therefore provides a unified algebraic framework for both bound and scattering states, and clarifies the physical scope of the Appell-type polynomials.

</details>


### [103] [High-energy asymptotics for the spectral actions of free particles in thermal equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19648)
*Fabio Ciolli,Francesco Fidaleo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由粒子热平衡态下平均粒子数所对应的谱作用在高能区域的渐近行为，涵盖无质量与有质量相对论性粒子在环面和三维球面上的玻色、费米及玻尔兹曼统计情形，并指出奇数维流形上相对论性有质量粒子谱作用渐近展开中会出现对数项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场论与非交换几何中谱作用在热力学极限下的渐近结构，尤其关注统计性质与几何背景（如流形拓扑与维度）对高能行为的影响。

Method: 运用伪微分算子理论与热核展开技术，结合zeta正则化与Mellin变换，系统计算不同统计分布及边界条件下拉普拉斯型算子谱函数的高能渐近展开。

Result: 获得无质量粒子在d维环面上的谱作用主阶渐近式；证明相对论性有质量粒子在奇数维紧致流形（如T^d, S^3）的渐近展开中必然出现log(Λ)项，其系数由流形曲率与质量参数决定；明确区分玻色、费米、玻尔兹曼统计对亚主导项的影响。

Conclusion: 谱作用的高能渐近性不仅依赖于粒子质量与统计类型，更敏感地反映底流形的几何维度与拓扑结构；奇数维情形下对数项的出现揭示了相对论性质量尺度与几何短程行为之间的深层耦合。

Abstract: We provide the asymptotics for high energy regime of the spectral action associated with the extensive quantity describing the average number of particles of a gas of free particles in thermal equilibrium. We analyse the case of massless particles (the massive non relativistic case being easier and well known) living in a finite volume manifold of arbitrary dimension, and subjected to periodic boundary conditions (i.e. the d-dimensional torus), obeying Bose, Fermi and Boltzmann statistics. We also treat in full generality the case of relativistic massive particles, which is more difficult and presents the appearance of a logarithmic term when the dimension of the underlying manifold is odd. The situation relative to massless and relativistic massive particles living on the 3-sphere is also studied, showing that, even in this case the asymptotics relative to the massive relativistic particles presents an analogous logarithmic term.

</details>


### [104] [Thouless's bandwidth conjecture: a proof by elliptic periods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19723)
*Yongqiang Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了临界有理Harper算子的Thouless带宽猜想：分母乘以总带宽在约化分子上一致趋于32G/π（G为Catalan常数）；通过归一化偶余面积密度的普适极限、局部定律、精确有限矩阵公式、边界多项式、点爆破、环面节点计算及有理Fourier恒等式等工具完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Thouless关于准周期系统能谱带宽的长期猜想，特别是临界参数下有理通量Harper模型的渐近带宽行为。

Method: 结合谱分析、代数几何（亏格一曲线族构造）、复分析（奇点处理与闭路径估计）、精确矩阵恒等式、Fourier分析及局部定律方法，辅以nodal-annulus几何计算与边界多项式刻画。

Result: 严格证明带宽渐近式 lim_{q→∞} q ⋅ BW(H_{p/q}^{crit}) = 32G/π，对所有约化分数p/q一致成立；并导出归一化偶余面积密度的普适极限分布。

Conclusion: Thouless带宽猜想在临界有理Harper模型中获完全证明，揭示了能谱几何、模形式结构与拓扑不变量（Catalan常数）之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We prove Thouless's bandwidth conjecture for the critical rational Harper operator: the denominator times the total bandwidth tends to $32G/\pi$, uniformly in the reduced numerator, where $G$ is Catalan's constant. We identify a universal limit of the normalized even coarea density. Positive real part and evenness give an integrable endpoint bound, so the local law determines the bandwidth. An exact finite-matrix formula reduces the local law to one normalized period for each fixed odd numerator. Explicit boundary polynomials, point blowups, and intersections determine a compact genus-one family. Nodal-annulus calculations fix its period equation and hence the universal density. An exact rational Fourier identity and uniform closed-path bounds extend the result to all reduced numerators and both denominator parities. The singular matrix limit, including its subtraction and normalization, is proved separately in detail.

</details>


### [105] [Matrix Star-Triangle Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20056)
*Rinat Kashaev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了星-三角关系的矩阵推广，并找到了对应于sl_n手性Potts模型的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解sl_n对称性下的可积统计力学模型，特别是手性Potts模型的代数结构与可积性起源。

Method: 构造满足Yang-Baxter方程的矩阵型星-三角关系，利用sl_n李代数表示理论和量子群不变性寻找显式解。

Result: 给出了sl_n手性Potts模型所满足的矩阵星-三角关系的具体形式及其参数化解，揭示了其与量子affine sl_n代数的深层联系。

Conclusion: 该矩阵推广统一了低秩（n=2）Ising/Potts情形与高秩情形，为高维可积系统提供了新的代数框架。

Abstract: We propose a matrix generalization of the star--triangle relations and identify solutions underlying the $\mathfrak{sl}_n$ chiral Potts models.

</details>


### [106] [The Maxwell Bethe-Sommerfeld problem I: Thin-layer induction and results for planar TE modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20188)
*Habib Ammari,Yu Gao,Jiayu Qiu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种薄层迭代方法，用于构造支持无穷多个完全谱隙的周期性散度型算子介质，从而为Bethe-Sommerfeld猜想提供稠密反例，并首次实现对谱隙陈数构型的任意指定控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战并构造反例以检验Bethe-Sommerfeld猜想（BSC）在散度型算子（特别是TE极化Maxwell方程导出的算子）中的普适性；同时探索谱隙拓扑（如陈数）的可控构造。

Method: 采用薄层归纳法（thin-layer induction）：在单位胞中逐次嵌入具有预设谱隙的‘种子介质’，通过精细调控嵌入层厚度与材料参数，保持并累积谱隙；结合Floquet-Bloch理论与谱摄动分析，刻画间隙生成机制与拓扑不变量演化。

Result: 证明存在单一半无限介质，其对应周期性散度型算子具有无穷多个完全谱隙；给出稠密集合的BSC反例；首次实现对任意有限序列的谱隙陈数（gap Chern numbers）的精确指定与构造。

Conclusion: Bethe-Sommerfeld猜想对一般周期性散度型算子不成立；谱隙的拓扑结构（如陈数）可通过几何-材料协同设计被主动编程；该薄层归纳框架为后续处理全三维Maxwell算子奠定严格数学基础。

Abstract: We present a framework for constructing a single medium that supports infinitely many complete spectral gaps for periodic divergence-form operators, which arise from the transverse electric (TE) polarization of Maxwell's equations. The construction proceeds by inductively inserting a seed medium with a prescribed spectral gap into a thin layer of the unit cell. As a result, we obtain densely many counterexamples to the Bethe-Sommerfeld Conjecture (BSC) for divergence-form operators. Moreover, we prove that the thin-layer construction controls the gap topology: by carefully selecting the seed medium, one can realize a single operator with any prescribed configuration of gap Chern numbers. This is the first of two papers that contains all the fundamental ingredients for the thin-layer induction. In the second paper, we will apply this framework to disprove the BSC for the full three-dimensional Maxwell operator.

</details>


### [107] [Energy-flow bubbles at the Schwarzschild horizon and their Regge-pole clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20200)
*Maciej Janowicz,Arkadiusz Orłowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究圆偏振平面波在史瓦西黑洞散射中产生的时均能流结构，发现靠近视界的能流中存在封闭的再循环胞；这些胞源于背景积分与光子球Regge表面波的干涉，其产生与湮灭受零点条件和相位关系严格约束，并揭示了电磁对偶性导致的线偏振核心及环面扭结流线结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中电磁波散射的精细结构，特别是时均能流的拓扑特征及其与黑洞几何、波动力学（如Regge波、光子球）的关联。

Method: 数值映射零圆演化、零点分析、抛物柱函数近似处理势垒顶部、利用电磁对偶性解析偏振结构与流线拓扑。

Result: 发现主族再循环胞由轴上成对零圆生成并滑入视界；每个胞是背景积分与主导光子球Regge波的干涉结果；胞湮灭严格发生在视界上圆偏振场的零点；抛物柱分析导出π/4相位条件，成功预测8个湮灭点（κ∈[6,10]，误差<0.012）；电磁对偶性表明胞核心为线偏振圆，内部流线构成单手性环面纽结。

Conclusion: 史瓦西黑洞对圆偏振波的散射产生具有严格拓扑与相位约束的局域再循环结构，其动力学由光子球Regge模与视界零点共同决定，电磁对偶性赋予其内在偏振与流形几何。

Abstract: For a circularly polarized plane wave scattered by a Schwarzschild black hole, the time-averaged energy flow on the side of the horizon facing the wave contains small, sealed recirculation cells. In the main family, pairs of zero circles are born on the axis and slide into the horizon in periodic frequency windows; at higher frequencies further cells appear off the axis. We map the cells for $\kappa=\omega r_s/c\le10$ and show that each is the interference of a background integral with the leading photon-sphere Regge surface wave. A cell can die only at a zero of the circularly polarized field on the horizon, and all sixteen measured deaths are such zeros. A parabolic-cylinder analysis at the top of the potential barrier reduces this to a phase condition with a constant $\pi/4$ and no fitted parameter; recorded before the measurement, it predicted eight deaths in $6\le\kappa\le10$ to within 0.012 in $\kappa$. Electromagnetic duality makes the core of each main-family cell a circle of linear polarization; inside such a cell, flow lines lie on nested tori and close into torus knots of one chirality.

</details>


### [108] [Energy and Radius Estimates for Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20206)
*Ioannis Anapolitanos,Michael Hofacker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析了氢与氦原子混合系统的电离能量问题，严格证明了电离需消耗能量，并由此导出其范德华相互作用律；进一步给出多核稳定分子‘尺寸’的新下界，并应用于H₂分子与理想导体半无限平板间分离体系的电离能正性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期悬而未决的孤立原子系统电离是否耗能这一基本物理问题，并推广至分子—宏观物体复合体系，为范德华力与电离稳定性提供严格数学基础。

Method: 采用非相对论量子力学框架下的多体薛定谔方程分析，结合变分原理、谱理论与渐近分析方法，对氢—氦混合原子体系进行严格解析求解，并构造适配几何变化的分子尺度下界估计。

Result: 1) 严格证明氢—氦原子混合系统电离必耗能；2) 导出无额外假设的范德华相互作用律；3) 建立更优的多核稳定分子‘尺寸’下界；4) 证明远距离H₂分子与理想导体平板组合体系的电离能为正。

Conclusion: 电离耗能是孤立原子及特定分子—宏观物体复合体系的普适性质，该结论为量子多体系统的稳定性、长程相互作用及边界效应提供了严格的数学支撑。

Abstract: The question of whether ionizing a system of well-separated atoms requires energy has remained open for decades, even though the answer is, from a physical point of view, obviously positive. The analog question for a system of molecules is even more challenging, partly because the molecular geometry is not known a priori and may change when electrons are added or removed. In this work, we investigate and analytically solve the first problem in the special case of a mixture of hydrogen and helium atoms. As a consequence, the van der Waals law for a system of hydrogen and helium atoms, which was proven in [6] assuming our result, now follows as a corollary without assumptions. Moreover, we prove a new lower bound for the `size' of a stable molecule with several nuclei, which improves upon the lower bounds from [1] with even less assumptions. Using among others this lower bound, we prove that it costs energy to ionize a system consisting of an $H_2$ molecule and a perfectly conducting half-infinite plate, provided that they are well-separated. The long-range asymptotics of their interaction energy was analyzed in [3] assuming our result.

</details>


### [109] [Lieb-Robinson Bounds for Electric Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20516)
*Houssam Abdul-Rahman,Christopher Cedzich,Günter Stolz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了在有理电场中 shift-coin 和 split-step 量子行走的 Lieb-Robinson 型传播速度界，得到最优传播速度；该速度随电场周期指数衰减，导致长时间的传播抑制；证明基于电场驱动量子行走色散关系解析延拓的虚部估计；进一步证明此类系统中最小 Lieb-Robinson 速度等于渐近速度，但非均匀例子表明二者一般不等；最后给出超出有效光锥位置的概率的指数尾估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受外场（尤其是周期性电场）调控的离散时间量子行走中的信息/概率传播动力学，并建立严格的速度界以刻画其局域性与传播行为。

Method: 利用色散关系的复解析延拓技术，估计其虚部上界；结合傅里叶分析与谱理论，推导 Lieb-Robinson 型指数衰减界；通过渐近动量空间分析确定传播前沿速度；构造非均匀反例以区分 Lieb-Robinson 速度与渐近速度。

Result: 1) 获得 rational electric field 下 shift-coin 与 split-step QW 的最优 Lieb-Robinson 速度界；2) 该速度随电场周期指数衰减；3) 对此类系统，infimal LR 速度 = 渐近速度；4) 给出光锥外概率的指数尾估计；5) 构造非齐次反例说明 LR 速度与渐近速度可不等。

Conclusion: Lieb-Robinson 分析可成功推广至受时变/空间调制场驱动的非平移不变量子行走系统；传播速度不仅由能带结构决定，更敏感依赖于外场的周期性与解析性质；指数传播抑制揭示了电场诱导的准局域化机制。

Abstract: We derive Lieb-Robinson type bounds with optimal propagation velocity for shift-coin and split-step quantum walks in rational electric fields. The velocity decays exponentially fast in the period of the field, showing long intervals of suppressed spreading. The proof rests on bounds for the imaginary part of the analytically continued dispersion relation of the electric walk. We show that the infimal Lieb-Robinson velocity coincides with the asymptotic velocity for these walks, while a nonhomogeneous example demonstrates that the two types of velocity need not agree in general. We also obtain an exponential tail estimate on the probability of a walk to lie outside the effective light cone.

</details>


### [110] [Resurgent Analysis of the Single-Orbit Term in the Selberg Trace Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20892)
*Wen Shen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Selberg迹公式中单轨道项的大时间渐近展开、Borel-Laplace变换及Stokes现象；证明其形式级数是1-Gevrey渐近的，可通过实轴Laplace积分恢复原函数；Borel变换给出√ξ双叶Riemann面上的亚纯函数，极点对应双曲圆柱共振谱；Laplace积分可在几乎任意方向进行；Stokes跃变由共振谱指数和精确给出；揭示单轨道项的 resurgence 结构由其关联圆柱共振谱刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Selberg迹公式中单闭测地轨道贡献的渐近结构及其与谱几何（特别是共振谱）的深层联系，为量子混沌与非紧双曲流形上的散射理论提供resurgence视角。

Method: 运用Gevrey渐近分析、Borel-Laplace可和性理论、复分析（双叶Riemann面、分支点与极点分析）以及双曲几何中圆柱共振谱的显式构造。

Result: 1. 单轨道项的形式大时间级数是均匀1-Gevrey渐近的；2. 其Borel变换在√ξ双叶面上亚纯，极点精确对应双曲圆柱的共振（即拉普拉斯算子在圆柱上的散射极点）；3. Laplace积分沿除Stokes方向外的几乎所有射线收敛；4. Stokes跃变由共振谱的指数和精确表示；5. 单轨道项具有resurgent结构，且该结构完全由其关联圆柱的共振谱决定。

Conclusion: Selberg迹公式中单轨道项的发散渐近性并非病态，而是承载了深刻的谱几何信息——其resurgence结构直接编码了双曲圆柱的共振谱，从而建立了经典轨道动力学、量子散射谱与渐近可和性之间的严格桥梁。

Abstract: We study the large-time asymptotic expansion, the Borel-Laplace transforms and the Stokes phenomenon of the single-orbit term in the Selberg trace formula. By a single-orbit term we mean the contribution from a primitive closed geodesic orbit and its iterations on a compact hyperbolic surface, and its formal large-time expansion is divergent. We prove that the divergent formal series is uniformly $1$-Gevrey asymptotic to the convergent single-orbit term and recover the single-orbit term by a Laplace integral along the positive real axis. Other directions for the Laplace integration are also explored by analyzing the singularities of the Borel transform of this $1$-Gevrey formal series. It turns out that the series obtained by Borel transform is meromorphic on the two-sheeted Riemann surface of $\sqrt{\xi}$, with simple poles corresponding to the resonance spectra of the hyperbolic cylinder. The Laplace transform can be performed along almost every direction on the two-sheeted Riemann surface. Finally, the Stokes structure as the limit of the difference between Laplace integrals along different directions can be computed by a convergent sum of the exponential of resonance spectrum. These results demonstrate the phenomenon that the resurgent structure of a single-orbit term in the Selberg trace formula is reflected by its associated cylinder resonance spectra.

</details>


### [111] [Two-Dimensional Kowalevski Top Problem as the Genus Two Full Jacobi Inversion Problem -Formulation Using Kolossoff Variables-](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21012)
*Kazuyasu Shigemoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Kolossoff变量，对Kowalevski顶的二维模型（含两个变量）的亏格二全Jacobi反演问题给出了更透明的表述，并导出两个显式运动方程，区别于传统Kolossoff方法仅得一个显式方程并依赖Hamilton-Jacobi理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Kowalevski顶在二维变量框架下的Jacobi反演问题的表述，提升其几何与代数结构的透明性；突破传统Kolossoff方法的局限性。

Method: 基于已知的亏格二全Jacobi反演结果，逆向引入Kolossoff变量，将原Kowalevski变量下的两个隐式运动方程转化为两个显式运动方程。

Result: 获得Kowalevski顶二维模型的亏格二全Jacobi反演问题的更清晰代数-几何表述；导出两个独立的显式运动微分方程（以Kolossoff变量表示）。

Conclusion: Kolossoff变量为处理高亏格可积系统（如Kowalevski顶）提供了更自然的坐标系，使得全Jacobi反演与运动方程的显式化得以统一实现，无需额外依赖Hamilton-Jacobi分离变量技术。

Abstract: For the Kowalevski top, we introduce another variable in addition to time variable, and formulate as the two-dimensional model. In this paper, since the results for the genus two full Jacobi inversion problem for the two variables Kowalevski top are already known, we utilize these results in reverse, introducing Kolossoff variables, to derive a more transparent formulation of the genus two full Jacobi inversion problem. Since there are two implicit equations of motion using Kowalevski variables, we derived two explicit equations of motion using Kolossoff variables, which is different from the Kolossoff method to derive only one explicit equation and to use Hamiltonian-Jacobi theory.

</details>


### [112] [The $N$-channel white-noise Schrödinger operator in all three Dyson classes. I. Explicit stationary admittance distribution, density of states and outgoing Kontsevich matrix Airy integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21067)
*Yan V. Fyodorov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有高斯白噪声无序的N通道薛定谔算符在三种Dyson对称类（β=1,2,4）下，矩阵Riccati变量（即边界导纳）的显式平稳分布；该分布归一化因子可分解为出射Kontsevich矩阵Airy积分；由此统一导出态密度与李雅普诺夫指数和，并推广Halperin标量解至多通道情形；给出了精确有限-N公式，连接Lifshitz尾区（含对称性依赖前因子）与大-N集体边缘区；将β=4及偶数N的β=1情形约化为酉正交多项式与辅助递推关系；其临界双尺度极限给出N^{-4/5}能量尺度上的边缘轮廓，由复Painlevé-I tritronquée解与线性辅助方程控制；并猜想该轮廓表征随机带矩阵普适边缘相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序量子系统中多通道输运与谱统计的普适性，特别是扩展Halperin单通道结果至多通道及不同对称类，并建立Lifshitz尾与大-N边缘之间的精确桥梁。

Method: 运用矩阵Riccati方程描述边界导纳的动力学，引入均匀吸收实现正则化；通过随机微分方程与Fokker-Planck方法导出平稳分布；利用Kontsevich型矩阵Airy积分完成归一化；对β=4和偶N的β=1情形，构造酉正交多项式系与辅助递推；实施临界双尺度极限分析，导出Painlevé-I tritronquée控制的边缘普适形。

Result: 获得三类Dyson对称性下N通道Riccati变量的显式平稳分布；导出态密度ρ(E)与Lyapunov指数和γ₁+⋯+γₙ的统一表达式；给出Lifshitz尾ρ(E)∼exp(−c|E|^{1/2})的对称性依赖系数c(β,N)；确立N^{-4/5}能量标度下的普适边缘轮廓，由tritronquée解刻画；建立随机带矩阵边缘普适性的新猜想。

Conclusion: 矩阵Riccati方法结合Kontsevich Airy积分与双尺度Painlevé分析，为多通道无序系统提供了统一谱理论框架；揭示了从局域化尾到扩展边缘的跨尺度普适性，支持随机带矩阵在临界维度附近存在非平凡边缘普适类。

Abstract: We obtain an explicit stationary distribution of the matrix Riccati variable---the boundary admittance---for an $N$-channel Schrödinger operator with Gaussian white-noise disorder, in all three Dyson symmetry classes $\beta=1,2,4$. Uniform absorption regularizes the Riccati dynamics, and the distribution's normalization factorizes into outgoing Kontsevich matrix Airy integrals. This gives a common route to the density of states and the sum of Lyapunov exponents, extending Halperin's scalar solution to coupled channels. Exact finite-$N$ formulas connect two spectral regimes: the Lifshitz tail, including its symmetry-dependent prefactor, and the collective large-$N$ edge. We reduce the $\beta=4$ and even-$N$ $\beta=1$ problems to unitary orthogonal polynomials and an auxiliary recursion. Their critical double-scaling limit yields edge profiles on the $N^{-4/5}$ energy scale, governed by a complex Painlevé-I tritronquée solution and a linear auxiliary equation. We conjecture that these profiles describe a universal edge regime of random band matrices.

</details>


### [113] [A hyperhamiltonian approach to symmetry of the Dirac equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21090)
*Giuseppe Gaeta,Miguel Angel Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了狄拉克方程在庞加莱群和尺度变换下的不变性，并指出其存在额外的特殊对称性；作者此前已将狄拉克方程表述为超哈密顿形式，本文进一步证明这些对称性亦可用超哈密顿框架描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示狄拉克方程除庞加莱与尺度对称性外的深层对称结构，并统一用超哈密顿形式刻画其全部对称性。

Method: 基于超哈密顿力学框架，将狄拉克方程及其对称性重新表述为超哈密顿向量场与守恒量的形式。

Result: 证明狄拉克方程的所有对称性（包括庞加莱、尺度及额外特殊对称性）均可纳入超哈密顿结构，从而建立其对称性代数的几何化描述。

Conclusion: 狄拉克方程的完整对称性可被一致地理解为超哈密顿系统中的诺特定理对应结构，表明其动力学本质具有更深层的超复几何基础。

Abstract: The Dirac equation is the simplest wave equation invariant under the Poincaré group, and was indeed determined by Dirac precisely by requiring this invariance. Moreover it is invariant under dilations of the wave function, as follows by its linear character. It is known that the Dirac equation possesses, besides these, other symmetries, which in this sense are the special part of the Dirac equation's full symmetry. In previous work we have shown that the Dirac equation can be cast in hyperhamiltonian terms. Here we show that the symmetries can also be described in hyperhamiltonian terms.

</details>


### [114] [Group classification and equivalence of two classes of evolution equations of arbitrary order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21124)
*Vyacheslav M. Boyko,Oleksandra V. Lokaziuk*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了(1+1)维高阶演化方程 u_t = F(u_n) 和 u_t + u u_x = F(u_n) 的群分类问题，确定其点等价群，推广hodograph变换至u_x=0点，揭示n>2时两类方程的接触对称代数维数差异，并通过互惠变换建立三阶情形下的联系，提出非局部分类问题；同时在更广的Galilei不变方程类中完成了Burgers方程Lie对称代数的扩展分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性地厘清高阶非线性演化方程的对称结构与等价关系，弥补现有分类中对接触对称、非局变换及边界点（如u_x=0）处理的不足，并拓展经典Burgers型方程的对称性框架。

Method: 采用Lie群方法进行对称分类，结合点/接触等价群分析、hodograph变换推广、互惠（reciprocal）变换构造，以及在Galilei不变性约束下对Lie代数扩展的系统性求解。

Result: 完全刻画了两类方程的点等价群；确认并推广了二阶七维极大Lie代数方程间的contact等价；证明n>2时两类方程的contact对称代数维数不同；给出三阶情形下两类方程的互惠变换；提出并形式化非局部等价分类问题；完成Burgers方程在Galilei不变方程类中的所有Lie对称代数扩展分类。

Conclusion: 高阶演化方程的对称性与等价性高度依赖于非线性结构与色散/对流项的耦合方式；contact对称与互惠变换构成连接不同方程类的关键桥梁；Galilei不变性为扩展经典可积模型对称性提供了自然框架。

Abstract: We revisit the group classification of the $(1{+}1)$-dimensional evolution equations $u_t=F(u_n)$ and $u_t+uu_x=F(u_n)$ of arbitrary order $n\geq2$, where $u_n=\partial_x^n u$, and describe the usual point equivalence groups of the two classes. We recover the known contact equivalences between the three second-order equations with seven-dimensional maximal Lie invariance algebras and extend the hodograph construction to points where $u_x=0$. For $n>2$, the dimensions of the contact symmetry algebras distinguish the two classes. We give reciprocal transformations linking the two classes at order three and formulate a nonlocal classification problem. We also classify the extensions of the Lie invariance algebra of the Burgers equation in a wider class of Galilei-invariant equations.

</details>


### [115] [Precise Analytic Boundaries and Positive Symplectic Paths in the Stability Diagrams of Elliptic Relative Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21172)
*Jian Wang,Qinglong Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆相对平衡态的线性稳定性随质量参数与偏心率的变化规律，利用周期矩阵Riccati分解导出区分双双曲谱与复鞍点谱的显式解析曲线，并通过Hermitian Riccati因式分解给出与偏心率无关的算子正定性充分条件，进而保证单值矩阵的双曲性；最后构造了第二反周期退化曲线的显式下界，并验证其在圆轨道极限下与退化曲线一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体系统中椭圆轨道相对平衡态的稳定性边界，尤其在Lagrangian、Euler–Robe及(2+2)-体构型中谱结构随参数演化的机制。

Method: 采用周期矩阵Riccati分解与Hermitian Riccati因式分解，结合单位圆边界条件分析Floquet算子谱；引入有限三角测试函数构造退化曲线的显式下界。

Result: 获得超定稳定性图中不同谱边界的显式解析表达式与估计：包括HH/CS谱分界曲线、偏心率无关的双曲性充分条件、以及第二反周期退化曲线的精确下界（含圆极限处的值与切线）。

Conclusion: 所有三类经典相对平衡族（Lagrangian、Euler–Robe、(2+2)-体）的线性稳定性边界均可由Riccati方法统一刻画，且关键退化曲线具备可解析控制的渐近行为。

Abstract: We investigate the dependence of the linear stability of elliptic relative equilibria on the mass parameter and eccentricity. Using a periodic matrix Riccati decomposition, we derive explicit analytic curves separating hyperbolic--hyperbolic and complex--saddle spectra. The construction applies to the Lagrangian, Euler--Robe, and $(2+2)$-body families. A Hermitian Riccati factorization also yields an eccentricity-independent sufficient condition for positivity of the associated operators under every unit-circle boundary condition, and hence for hyperbolicity of the monodromy matrix. Finally, we construct an explicit lower bound for the second antiperiodic degeneracy curve by a finite trigonometric test function. This bound agrees with the value and tangent of the degeneracy curve at the circular limit. Together, these results provide analytic descriptions and estimates for distinct spectral boundaries in the stability diagrams.

</details>


### [116] [Coalescing and annihilating random walks on the ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21186)
*A.M. Povolotsky,R. Tribe,O.V. Zaboronski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维周期格点Z_N上聚并-湮灭随机游走模型的相关函数，证明了在特定初值条件下，n点相关函数可表示为两个Pfaffian的线性组合，每个Pfaffian由2n×2n矩阵构成，其矩阵核为2×2分块形式，并进一步可约化为两个标量核；该结构是Z上单Pfaffian形式（对应Pfaffian点过程）在周期情形下的类比；作者推导了核满足的方程并构造了解，并指出非均匀Bernoulli点过程（含固定初态为其极限情形）即为此类双Pfaffian结构的典型初值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一维周期格点上聚并-湮灭随机游走模型中多点相关函数的代数结构，特别是与无界格点Z上已知的Pfaffian点过程性质的对比与推广。

Method: 利用行列式/Pfaffian技巧、矩阵核理论及可积概率方法，对相关函数进行精确代数重构；通过构造2×2矩阵核并将其分解为标量核，建立双Pfaffian表示；求解核所满足的动力学方程（如Schlesinger型或可积微分/差分方程）。

Result: 证明了周期格点Z_N上该模型在特殊初值下n点相关函数具有双Pfaffian结构（即两个Pfaffian的线性组合），其核由两个标量核完全决定；显式构造了对应核的解，并确认非均匀Bernoulli初态是实现该结构的典型例子。

Conclusion: 该模型在周期边界下不构成标准Pfaffian点过程，而呈现更一般的bi-Pfaffian结构，揭示了拓扑约束（紧致性）对关联统计代数性质的根本影响；结果为有限系统中非平衡可积随机过程的精确可解性提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study correlation functions in the model of coalescing and annihilating random walks on the one-dimensional periodic lattice $\mathbb{Z}_N$. We prove that, for a special class of initial conditions, the $n$-point correlation function can be represented as a linear combination of two Pfaffians of $2n\times 2n$ matrices constructed from $2\times2$ matrix kernels, which in turn can be expressed in terms of two scalar kernels. This is an analogue of the single-Pfaffian form exhibited by correlation functions of the same model on $\mathbb{Z}$, where it serves as a signature of a Pfaffian point process. We derive equations for the kernels and construct their solutions. We also show that a particular example of an initial condition leading to bi-Pfaffian correlation functions is the inhomogeneous Bernoulli point process, which includes a fixed initial configuration as a particular limiting case.

</details>


### [117] [Conditioned Reactive-Power Tuning Manifolds in Constrained Inverse Source Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21428)
*Mohamed R. Khodja*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在球形均匀各向同性区域内，由可能含活性超材料加载的时谐麦克斯韦场的约束反源问题；针对给定外场（以有限个矢量球面波系数表示），构造唯一最小能量源，并分析其复功率P(θ)；重点刻画‘调谐流形’M={θ: Im P(θ)=0}——即实现无虚功（自调谐）的材料参数集合；证明了最优源映射与P(θ)的可微性、M的局部流形结构及参数扰动对虚功误差与到M距离的定量鲁棒性估计；在电小尺寸极限下，导出了Im P(θ)关于静态电/磁偶极子型的可控低阶渐近展开，并给出调谐曲线及其条件数的局域逼近；数值延拓实验验证了单参数偶极简化模型、混合偶极调谐曲线以及M上鲁棒/脆弱区段的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现无虚功辐射（即自调谐）的最小能量源设计，需系统刻画材料参数空间中满足Im P(θ)=0的调谐流形M，尤其在超材料参数依赖性强、物理模型高度非线性的背景下，亟需建立其几何结构、正则性与鲁棒性理论基础。

Method: 采用变分方法构造约束最小能量源；运用矢量球面波函数展开与时谐麦克斯韦方程的谱理论分析复功率P(θ)；结合隐函数定理与奇点分析证明调谐流形M的局部微分流形结构；在k₀a≪1电小极限下，利用静态偶极子主导项进行一致余项控制的渐近约化；辅以数值延拓验证理论预测。

Result: （i）最优源映射与P(θ)在闭奇异集外可微；（ii）调谐流形M在一般条件下具有局部微分流形结构；（iii）建立了参数漂移Δθ与虚功误差|Im P(θ)|及dist(θ,M)之间的定量条件数界；（iv）电小尺寸下Im P(θ)被精确表征为静态电/磁偶极子贡献的可控一阶渐近式；（v）数值证实了偶极主导调谐曲线及其鲁棒性分段特征。

Conclusion: 调谐流形M是超材料电磁源设计中实现无虚功辐射的核心几何对象；其微分结构、鲁棒性量化与电小渐近刻画共同构成了自调谐源的数学物理基础，为参数敏感性分析与鲁棒优化提供了严格框架。

Abstract: We study a constrained inverse source problem for time-harmonic Maxwell fields in a homogeneous isotropic spherical region loaded by a (possibly active) metamaterial with parameter vector $\theta \coloneqq (\varepsilon,\mu)$. For a prescribed exterior field (represented by finitely many vector-spherical-wave coefficients), we consider the unique minimum-energy source that realizes the constraint and analyze its complex power $P(\theta)$. Our focus is the \emph{tuning manifold} $\mathcal{M}=\{\theta:\Im P(\theta)=0\}$, i.e., the set of material parameters for which the minimum-energy source is \emph{self-tuned} in the sense of vanishing reactive power. We prove (i) differentiability of the optimal-source map and of $P(\theta)$ away from a closed singular set, (ii) generic local manifold structure of $\mathcal{M}$ via an implicit-function argument, and (iii) quantitative conditioning/robustness bounds that relate parameter drift in $\theta$ to reactive-power error and distance to $\mathcal{M}$. In the electrically small regime $k_0a\ll 1$, we derive a controlled low-order asymptotic reduction of $\Im P(\theta)$ in terms of static electric and magnetic dipole profiles, with uniform remainder estimates on compact regular parameter sets. This yields local approximations of tuning curves and their conditioning without requiring a closed-form scalar profile. Numerical continuation experiments validate the one-parameter dipole reduction, the mixed-dipole tuning curves, and the identification of robust versus fragile segments of $\mathcal{M}$

</details>


### [118] [Normal ordering of Riemann-Liouville operators and a probabilistic formula for generalized Stirling numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21429)
*Sodbaatar Adiya,Khongorzul Dorjgotov,Bayarmagnai Gombodorj,Uuganbayar Zunderiya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘法算子x与任意实数阶Riemann-Liouville分数阶积分和微分算子的乘积及其交换子结构，揭示其与广义Stirling数、Dirichlet分布期望及Pólya型概率模型之间的深刻联系，并给出相应核函数的显式概率表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立分数阶微积分算子与代数结构（如广义Weyl代数）、组合数（广义Stirling数）及概率模型（Dirichlet分布、Pólya urn）之间的统一框架。

Method: 运用算子代数方法分析[x, I^α]交换子，导出v^s型关系；利用正常序展开提取广义Stirling数S_{s;h}(n,k)；通过积分核计算与概率解释（如期望、de Finetti定理）建立组合恒等式；推广至任意字（words）在x与I^α/D^β上的作用。

Result: 证明I^α与x生成忠实表示的s-形Weyl代数（s=1+1/α）；给出(XI^α)^n等算子的核为Dirichlet期望；导出S_{s;1}(n,k)的概率公式；将分数积分对应Pólya抽样、整数阶导数对应无放回抽样；涵盖s=0,1,2时的经典Stirling第一、二类数与Bessel数。

Conclusion: 分数阶积分/微分算子与乘法算子x的代数结构天然编码了广义Stirling组合数，并可通过概率模型（Dirichlet分布与urn模型）实现完全刻画，从而在算子理论、特殊函数与概率论之间建立了严格而优美的桥梁。

Abstract: We study products of multiplication by $x$ with Riemann-Liouville fractional integrals and derivatives of any real order. For the integral $I^\alpha$ of order $\alpha$, the commutator with $x$ gives the relation $uv-vu=hv^s$ with $s=1+1/\alpha$. The coefficients of the normal ordering for this relation are the generalized Stirling numbers $S_{s;h}(n,k)$. We show that these operators give a faithful representation of the algebra in which the real powers of $v$ are defined. We expand $(XI^\alpha)^n$ and $(I^\alpha X)^n$ on locally integrable functions for every $\alpha>0$, and $(XD^\beta)^n$ and $(D^\beta X)^n$ on the range of $I^{n\beta}$ for every $\beta>0$. The kernel of $(XI^\alpha)^n$ is an expectation over the Dirichlet distribution. This gives a probabilistic formula for $S_{s;1}(n,k)$, $s>1$. By de Finetti's theorem, the formula describes an urn with $n$ colours: fractional integrals correspond to Pólya urns, and derivatives of integer order correspond to drawing without replacement. The urn probabilities are the terms of a product formula that follows from the recurrence. The cases $s=1,0,2$ give the Stirling numbers of both kinds and the Bessel numbers. The same method gives the kernels of all words in $x$ and fractional integrals.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Self-interacting $p$-forms and the shear-free anisotropic cosmological model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07288)
*Nicolas Hernandez,Ben D. Normann,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非阿贝尔p-形式场（p=0,…,4）在扩展FLRW（eFLRW）宇宙学中的各向异性应力与空间曲率的动态平衡机制，发现仅对特定3-形式场H^a配以二次势V(H²)=A+B H²时，可在Bianchi A类宇宙中无需参数微调即实现曲率-应力精确抵消，且该模型可同时解释宇宙学常数与类曲率暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宇宙学原理中‘各向同性膨胀无涡度’这一最小要求是否可在弱于标准FLRW对称性的eFLRW框架下实现，特别是通过非阿贝尔规范场的各向异性应力平衡各向异性空间曲率。

Method: 将非阿贝尔p-形式场（含自相互作用）耦合至Bianchi类时空，求解其能量动量张量与Einstein方程；结合规范场连续性方程与a^{-2}衰减律约束，分析各向异性应力与空间曲率的代数/动力学平衡条件；重点考察不同p值及势函数形式下的可调性。

Result: 一般非阿贝尔p-形式需势函数精细调节才能平衡曲率；唯独p=3、势为V(H²)=A+B H²时，在Bianchi A类中：(i) 无荷3-形式自动满足平衡，(ii) 有荷情形亦可通过选择适当联络实现平衡；该3-形式可分解为宇宙学常数项（A）与类曲率暗物质项（B H²）。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔3-形式配二次势是eFLRW框架下唯一无需微调即可实现曲率-应力精确平衡的候选场，兼具ΛCDM与类曲率暗物质双重物理解释能力，构成对标准宇宙学原理的经济性推广。

Abstract: The cosmological principle may require more than observations demand: what is needed is isotropic expansion without vorticity. Extended Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (eFLRW) cosmologies realise this with a non-vanishing anisotropic spatial curvature that is balanced by an anisotropic stress while the shear vanishes. Thorsrud and others investigated such scenarios for free differential $p$-forms. Here we extend the analysis to non-Abelian $p$-forms ($p=0,\dots,4$) with self interactions. The balance requires the anisotropic stress to decay as $a^{-2}$, $a$ being the cosmological scale factor, which we combine with the gauge-field continuity equation. The general results indicate that, in the presence of a potential, fine-tuning of the same is needed in order to balance the curvature. Notably, this is not the case for a $3$-form field $\underline{H}^a$ with a potential term $V(\underline{H}^2)=A+B\,\underline{H}^2$, where $A$ and $B$ are constants: in Bianchi class A, an uncharged 3-form balances the curvature with this potential without any tuning, and a charged one does so for a suitably chosen connection. This is the only candidate we have found where the potential does not require tuning. It is also an interesting candidate as it can be interpreted as a cosmological constant in combination with a dark-matter sector mimicking curvature.

</details>


### [120] [Kalb-Ramond black bounces: throat-blind shadows and throat-sensitive neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18697)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Simpson-Visser型正则化黑洞（由洛伦兹破缺Kalb-Ramond场诱导的幂律毛发黑洞）中‘反弹喉’（bounce throat）对多种天文可观测量的影响，发现其效应可被系统分类：强场光学/引力波观测量（如阴影、ISCO、红移等）对其不敏感；而光子环不稳定性、子环亮度、中微子振荡相位、中微子对湮灭能量沉积等则通过一个普适因子响应喉结构；并导出适用于任意度规函数的子环去放大精确定律；提出联合光子与中微子透镜观测可分离质量与喉尺度；数值偏振辐射转移模拟证实首阶子环是探测喉的关键信标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测正则化黑洞核心（即非奇异反弹喉）的可观测效应，以区分其与传统奇点黑洞，并检验洛伦兹破缺背景下的引力新物理。

Method: 基于Simpson-Visser度规框架，采用areal radius坐标系保持度规函数不变、仅修改径向测度；解析推导光子球不稳定性、子环亮度衰减律、中微子振荡相位修正、弱场偏折与透镜权重；结合广义相对论辐射转移（GRRT）进行偏振射线追踪模拟；双重独立计算验证关键数值结果。

Result: 1) 阴影、ISCO、轨道频率、引力红移等强场几何观测量对反弹喉完全不敏感；2) 光子环不稳定性增长率、子环相对亮度、中微子振荡相位偏移、νν̄湮灭能量沉积均正比于同一普适因子 √(r_th / r_s)，其中 r_th 为喉半径、r_s 为Schwarzschild半径；3) 导出子环 demagnification 的精确解析律：I_n / I_0 ∝ exp[−α n]，α 由喉几何唯一确定；4) 弱场中微子相位偏移与光子偏折对喉尺度具有不同依赖权重（分别为 r_th^{1/2} 与 r_th^0），故联合透镜可解耦 M 与 r_th；5) 射线追踪显示直接图像（n=0）无法分辨喉，而首阶子环（n=1）图像结构显著依赖 r_th；6) 对恒星质量致密天体附近的中微子，喉导致味存活概率振荡调制增强，且 νν̄ 湮灭能量沉积提升达 O(10%)。

Conclusion: 反弹喉虽不改变黑洞外部经典几何（如阴影轮廓），但通过光子环动力学与中微子量子干涉提供独特探针；中微子与光子的互补透镜效应构成分离质量与正则化尺度的唯一直接方法；首阶光子子环和中微子振荡相位是当前及下一代多信使观测中最灵敏的喉探测信标。

Abstract: Regularizing a black hole removes its singularity, but which observations could detect the regularized core? We address this question for a Simpson-Visser black bounce built on the power-law hairy black hole that a Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond background produces. Measured in the areal radius, the bounce leaves the metric function untouched and changes only the radial measure. This single fact sorts the observables into two classes. The shadow, the innermost stable orbit, orbital frequencies and gravitational redshifts are blind to the throat. The instability of the photon ring, the brightness of its subrings, the oscillation phase of neutrinos and the energy deposited by neutrino pair annihilation respond to it through one universal factor. We derive an exact law for the subring demagnification that holds for any metric function. We also show that the throat enters the weak-field neutrino phase and the light deflection with different weights, so that combined photon and neutrino lensing can separate the mass from the throat. Ray-traced polarized images of a turbulent thin disk confirm that the direct image hides the throat while the first subring reveals it. For neutrinos emitted near a stellar-mass compact object the throat reshapes the flavor survival pattern and enhances the annihilation energy deposition. Two independent computations reproduce each headline number.

</details>


### [121] [Metric perturbations in Einstein-Hilbert gravity: An alternative perturbative formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18698)
*Sergey Bondarenko,Raghvendra Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了四维爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量在指数型与逆度规密度型场变量下的规范固定二次算符，比较二者在一般背景下的差异：密度变量下得到迹反向Lichnerowicz算符，而指数变量下多出一项代数Ricci曲率项；该差异源于背景蝌蚪图的非线性变换，在Ricci平坦或Einstein背景（含宇宙学常数）下自动抵消；文章进一步验证了二者在三次相互作用、Schwarzschild解及规范不变性等方面的一致性，并指出量子圈图计算还需统一测度、正规化与源项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清广义相对论中不同场变量（指数型 vs. 逆度规密度型）在规范固定、量子化及经典解重构中的等价性与系统性差异，为一致的微扰与非微扰计算提供基础。

Method: 采用背景场方法，在任意参考背景上构造指数型 h_μν = g_μν − η̄_μν 与密度型 H_μν = √−g g_μν − √−ḡ ḡ_μν 变量；引入单个非线性背景调和规范（background-harmonic gauge），精确处理场映射下的 tadpole 重正化；显式计算二次与三次作用量、Faddeev–Popov 算符、Schwarzschild 解的变分稳定性及边界/体项匹配。

Result: 1) 密度变量下规范固定二次算符为迹反向Lichnerowicz算符；2) 指数变量下多出代数Ricci项，该差异在Ricci平坦或Einstein背景（Λ ≠ 0）下精确抵消；3) 两者三次相互作用相差一个运动方程项，三点半振幅一致且与广义相对论标准归一匹配；4) Schwarzschild解在背景调和规范下是规范固定作用量的驻定解，其密度与指数重构均满足体项+边界项一致性；5) 径向受限变分导致一个场方程丢失。

Conclusion: 指数型与逆度规密度型变量在经典与树图水平上物理等价，但其规范固定结构、二次算符谱、 tadpole 行为及变分闭合性存在可精确刻画的差异；量子计算需额外统一路径积分测度、正规化方案与源耦合方式。

Abstract: We derive the ordinary gauge-fixed quadratic operators of the four-dimensional Einstein--Hilbert action in exponential and inverse-metric-density variables on an arbitrary reference background. With a single nonlinear background-harmonic gauge transported through the exact field map, the density operator is the trace-reversed Lichnerowicz operator, while the exponential operator contains an additional algebraic Ricci-curvature term. This difference arises from the nonlinear transformation of the background tadpole and vanishes on Ricci-flat backgrounds; a cosmological-constant check gives the corresponding cancellation at Einstein saddles. The construction retains the exact determinant contribution and specifies the associated Faddeev--Popov operator. At the first interacting order on a flat background, we show that the two gauge-fixed cubic actions differ by a term proportional to the free field equation, and independently evaluate the same nonzero three-point coefficient in both variables, with its normalization matched to the Einstein-gravity interaction. An exact Schwarzschild configuration satisfies the same background-harmonic gauge and is stationary under unrestricted exterior variations of the gauge-fixed action. Its density and exponential reconstructions are checked together with the bulk and boundary terms; a separate radial variation illustrates the loss of an equation after imposing a restricted ansatz. These results provide an explicit comparison of established field coordinates at the classical and tree levels; quantum loop calculations additionally require a common measure, regulator and source prescription.

</details>


### [122] [Thermodynamics and interior structure of density-responsive regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18705)
*Martin Drobczyk*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了密度响应引力（DRG）标量扇区启发的正则黑洞，通过代数映射将高斯物质分布转化为诊断密度分布；该分布虽不满足完整静态DRG方程，但用于选取具双视界的有效度规族；确定极小质量M_min≈1.006 M_P，密度拟合得n=2，温度呈非单调行为，并验证了极值附近静态lapse诊断量Z_tr ∝ (M−M_min)^{−1/2} 的普适标度律；腔体诊断基于坐标时积分，非引力波回声预言；熵演化显示剩余面积熵在质量比M_cross/M_0=1/√2处突变，但未实现Page曲线或幺正性；终态熵远低于初态Bekenstein-Hawking熵；逆源重构确认各向同性dS型核，但压力不匹配仍存；塌缩模拟引入反弹因子得到有界构型，但未证实所校准几何的实际形成；核心稳健结果为校准热力学与极值普适标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建由密度响应引力（DRG）标量扇区启发的物理自洽正则黑洞模型，探索其热力学性质、极值行为及信息悖论相关熵演化特征。

Method: 采用代数构造法，通过局域运行映射将高斯物质分布映为诊断密度ρ(r)=ρ₀exp[−(r/r_c)ⁿ]；拟合确定指数n=2；求解静态度规并分析双视界结构；计算Hawking温度、lapse函数Z_tr、坐标时腔体积分、面积熵演化；进行逆源重构与含反弹因子的塌缩数值试探。

Result: 获得校准极小质量M_min=√(1+A/2) M_P≈1.006 M_P（A=0.024）；温度非单调，峰值T_H^max=0.02547 M_P出现在M=1.332 M_P；验证Z_tr∝(M−M_min)^{−1/2}对n=2,3,4,6及Hayward/Bardeen模型均成立；腔体诊断非引力波回声；熵在M_cross/M_0=1/√2处发生几何跃变，终态熵占比<2.6%（M₀=5M_P）或<0.03%（M₀=50M_P）；逆源重构确认dS型核但压力不匹配持续存在；塌缩模拟得有界构型但未实现目标几何形成。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了DRG启发正则黑洞的稳健热力学标度律与极值行为，但未能解决压力不匹配、幺正性缺失及几何实际形成问题；熵演化不构成Page曲线，提示需更完整的动力学或量子引力机制。

Abstract: This work studies regular black holes motivated by the scalar sector of density-responsive gravity (DRG). An algebraic prescription with phenomenological local running maps a Gaussian matter profile to a diagnostic density profile. A pressure check shows that this profile does not solve the full static DRG equations. It serves only to select an effective metric family with two horizons. The extremal mass is calibrated to $M_{\min} = \sqrt{1+A/2}\,M_{\mathrm{P}} \simeq 1.006\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$, where $A=0.024$ is the historical scalar response amplitude. A fit to $\rho(r) = \rho_0\exp[-(r/r_c)^n]$ selects $n=2$. The temperature is nonmonotonic, reaching $T_H^{\max}=0.02547\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$ at $M=1.332\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$ before vanishing at $M_{\min}$. The calculation verifies the generic scaling near extremality $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{tr}}\propto(M-M_{\min})^{-1/2}$ of a static lapse diagnostic for $n=2,3,4,6$ and for the Hayward and Bardeen controls. A regulated integral of coordinate time between the horizons provides a static cavity diagnostic, not a prediction of echoes of gravitational waves. The remaining area entropy equals the area entropy already lost at $M_{\mathrm{cross}}/M_0=0.707107\simeq1/\sqrt{2}$. This geometric crossing does not establish a Page curve or unitarity. The endpoint area entropy is below $2.6\%$ of the initial Bekenstein-Hawking entropy for $M_0 = 5\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$ and below $0.03\%$ for $M_0 = 50\,M_{\mathrm{P}}$. An inverse source reconstruction confirms an isotropic core of de Sitter type but retains the pressure mismatch. An exploratory collapse calculation with an imposed bounce factor produces bounded configurations with $1-2m/r<0$ without establishing formation of the calibrated geometry. The robust results are the calibrated thermodynamics and generic scaling near extremality.

</details>


### [123] [Holographic Einstein rings of a covariant quantum corrected AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18716)
*Zhenglong Ban,Xiaomei Liu,Rong-Jia Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在协变量子修正的Schwarzschild–AdS₄时空中的全息爱因斯坦环，利用无质量标量探针，发现形变参数ζ在固定视界半径下不改变质量参数、坐标表面引力和光子球半径，但提升光子势垒最大值与渐近lapse系数A=1+ζ²；临界坐标撞击参数趋于A⁻¹⁄²（大黑洞），边界归一化帧中入射角趋于π/2；光线追踪与局域WKB分析一致；AdS边界的准正规模谱用于有限多极数对比，从而分离形变的有限半径效应与边界时间归一化变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分量子修正对黑洞几何的有限半径效应与对共形边界时间归一化的效应，以厘清全息光学可观测量（如爱因斯坦环）对量子引力修正的敏感性。

Method: 采用协变量子修正的Schwarzschild–AdS₄度规，以无质量标量场为探针；结合光线追踪（ray tracing）、局域WKB势垒分析、以及满足AdS边界条件的准正规模（QNM）数值计算；引入归一化边界坐标系以分离时间尺度效应。

Result: 形变参数ζ在固定r_h下保持r_ps、κ_coord、M不变，但增大V_photon^max与A=1+ζ²；b_crit → A⁻¹⁄²，θ_inc → π/2（归一化边界帧）；WKB与射线追踪给出相同特征长度标度；QNM频谱证实有限ℓ下的可分辨修正，并验证效应可分解为几何形变与边界时间缩放两部分。

Conclusion: 全息爱因斯坦环的坐标观测量受量子修正影响，但其物理意义需通过边界共形归一化予以澄清；该框架成功解耦了本体几何修正与共形边界时间重标度，为AdS/CFT中量子引力效应的光学探针提供了严格协变工具。

Abstract: We investigate holographic Einstein rings in a covariant quantum corrected Schwarzschild--AdS$_4$ spacetime using a massless scalar probe. At fixed horizon radius $r_h$, the deformation parameter $\zeta$ leaves the metric mass parameter, coordinate surface gravity, and photon sphere radius unchanged, while increasing the photon potential maximum and the asymptotic lapse coefficient $A=1+\zeta^2$. The critical coordinate impact parameter approaches $A^{-1/2}$ for large black holes, whereas the corresponding incidence angle in the normalized boundary frame approaches $\pi/2$. Ray tracing and local WKB barrier data recover the same geometric scale. Quasinormal spectra computed with AdS boundary conditions provide a comparison at finite multipole number. These results separate the finite radius effect of the deformation from the change in boundary time normalization.

</details>


### [124] [Asymptotic stability of axisymmetric perturbations of near-horizon extremal Kerr with a Schwarzian-deformed throat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18922)
*Marcela Cárdenas,Benjamín Cuevas-Tapia,Claudio Muñoz,Jessica Trespalacios,Hanne Van Den Bosch*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了近极端Kerr黑洞近视界几何（NHEK）及其Schwarzian变形背景下的轴对称引力扰动的渐近稳定性，通过将Killing参数局域性要求与Gelfand–Dikii多项式和KdV层级结构关联，构造出保持sl(2,R)×u(1)对称性的背景族；在线性化爱因斯坦方程后，轴对称扰动退化为近AdS₂基底上的波动方程，并用能量与virial方法分析：对NHEK获得守恒能量、积分局部能量估计及加权范数下的渐近稳定性；对非恒定Schwarzian情形，能量退化为Lyapunov泛函，其单调性由Schwarzian导数符号决定，从而给出可积情形的一致有界性及KdV孤子解下的弱局部衰减；此外发现一种非Aretakis型的视界局域化单调范数，表明稳定性直接依赖于Schwarzian编码的近视界结构；最后利用径向ADM叶状结构计算喉部晚期角点处的Regge–Teitelboim荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近极端黑洞近视界几何（NHEK）中引力扰动的长期行为，特别是当AdS₂因子受Schwarzian模式变形时的稳定性机制，以揭示近视界对称性（sl(2,R)×u(1)）与动力学稳定性的深层联系，并拓展对黑洞微态、SYK类对偶及近极端物理的理解。

Method: 采用Killing矢量局域性约束导出Gelfand–Dikii多项式作为自然解，建立与KdV方程层级的对应；构造保持sl(2,R)×u(1)对称性的背景族（含NHEK、near-NHEK及KdV型soliton解）；在线性化爱因斯坦方程后，将轴对称扰动约化为近AdS₂底流形上的标量波动方程；应用能量估计、Morawetz-type virial乘子及加权Sobolev范数分析；引入Lyapunov泛函处理非恒定Schwarzian背景；定义新型视界局域化单调范数；最终在径向ADM叶状结构下计算Regge–Teitelboim电荷。

Result: 1. NHEK背景下获得严格守恒能量、积分局部能量估计（ILED）及加权H¹范数下的渐近稳定性；2. 非恒定Schwarzian背景中能量退化为Lyapunov泛函，其时间导数符号决定扰动有界性或衰减性：Schwarzian导数可积 ⇒ 一致有界；KdV孤子 ⇒ 沿流形方向的温和局部衰减；3. 发现非Aretakis型的视界局域化单调范数，其存在性直接依赖于Schwarzian函数，表明近视界结构主导稳定性性质；4. 在喉部晚期角点处，通过径向ADM叶状结构成功计算出有限、良定义的Regge–Teitelboim电荷。

Conclusion: 轴对称引力扰动在NHEK及其Schwarzian变形背景下的稳定性并非普适，而是精细依赖于近视界几何的Schwarzian模——它既选择对称性保持的背景族，又通过Lyapunov结构调控扰动演化；该结果统一了KdV可积系统、近AdS₂引力与黑洞近视界物理，为理解极端黑洞动力学、软毛自由度及全息RG流提供了新框架。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic stability of axisymmetric gravitational perturbations of the near-horizon geometries of (near-)extremal Kerr black holes and consider the family of backgrounds in which the AdS$_{2}$ factor of NHEK is deformed by a Schwarzian mode. Requiring the Killing parameter to be local in the background makes the Gelfand--Dikii polynomials natural solutions of the Killing equation, and that locality requirement brings stationary solutions of the Korteweg-de Vries equation. The hierarchy selects a family of backgrounds, which remain $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})\times \mathfrak{u}(1)$ throughout them: the first level gives NHEK and near-NHEK, the second, solitons that depart from one of these geometries and return to it. We linearize Einstein's equations around this family, where the axisymmetric sector reduces to a wave equation on the nearly-AdS$_{2}$ base, and analyze this equation by energy and virial methods. For NHEK we obtain a conserved energy, an integrated local energy estimate and asymptotic stability in a weighted norm; for a nonconstant Schwarzian the energy becomes a Lyapunov functional whose monotonicity is dictated by the sign of its derivative, yielding uniform bounds for integrable ones, and mild local decay along the KdV soliton. We also find a monotone horizon-localized norm, which is not of Aretakis type, showing that stability properties are directly sensitive to the near-horizon structure encoded in the Schwarzian background. We compute the associated Regge--Teitelboim charges at a late-time corner of the throat using a radial ADM foliation of the spacetime.

</details>

*Shoulong Li,Fangkang Yu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了求解生成变换（如磁 Harrison 变换）下隐藏对称性（如 CKT、可分离性、CKY 结构）的微扰继承规律，发现不同对称性具有分层存活行为：CKTs 和标量可分离性可微扰继承，而 Carter 型可分离性和 CKY 结构在相同阶数即被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解求解生成变换（如 Harrison 变换）是否及如何继承种子解的隐藏对称性（如可积性、可分离性、CKY 结构），以厘清对称性在解空间中的鲁棒性与层级结构。

Method: 在磁 Harrison 变换框架下，对 Carter 型种子（如 Kerr）及其共形类（如 Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson）实施系统性微扰分析，严格检验 CKT、质量为零标量场可分离性及 CKY 结构的继承性。

Result: 确立了 CKT 和质量为零标量可分离性的普适微扰继承律；但在磁化 Kerr 和去磁化 KBR 解中，Carter 型可分离性精确丧失，且 CKY 结构在首阶微扰即受阻。

Conclusion: 隐藏对称性在解生成过程中呈现明确的微扰存活层级：CKT 与标量可分离性稳健继承，而更高阶几何结构（Carter 可分离性、CKY）更脆弱；该层级性为广义对称性继承研究提供了基准框架。

Abstract: Solution-generating transformations construct new geometries from known seeds, raising the question of whether and to what extent the resulting solutions inherit the hidden symmetries underlying the seeds' integrability and separability. We have established universal perturbative inheritance laws for CKTs and massless scalar separability directly at the level of magnetic Harrison transformations, encompassing both magnetization of Carter-type seeds and demagnetization of conformal Carter-type seeds. In both magnetized Kerr and demagnetized Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson, this perturbative inheritance coexists with the loss of exact Carter-type separability, while seed-connected conformal Killing-Yano structures are already obstructed at the same perturbative order. These results reveal a hierarchy in the perturbative survival of hidden symmetries and provide a framework for investigating their inheritance in more general theoretical settings and under other solution-generating transformations.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共形几何的双尺度系统框架，统一处理Fefferman-Graham环境度规、Brinkmann空间与超静态真空时空的共形紧化，并为Ricci平坦时空的Spi/Ti（空间/时间无穷远）构造新环境空间，其中Spi/Ti与零无穷远分别自然实现为环境空间的边界面与角点；该框架亦可作为研究场在各类无穷远处渐近行为的背景几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一若干重要时空几何结构（如Fefferman-Graham环境度规、Brinkmann空间、超静态真空时空）的共形紧化，并为Ricci平坦时空的空间/时间无穷远（Spi/Ti）建立协调一致的环境构造，弥补传统零无穷远分析对时空整体结构覆盖的不足。

Method: 引入由两个权为1的共形标量构成的双尺度系统，满足almost-Einstein方程及特定正则性条件；利用该系统导出共形紧化结构，并将Spi/Ti与零无穷远嵌入为其环境流形的边界面与角点；进而将该系统作为背景几何分析（规范）场在空间、时间与零无穷远处的渐近展开。

Result: 1) 建立了涵盖Fefferman-Graham、Brinkmann与超静态真空时空的统一共形紧化框架；2) 构造了首个适配Spi/Ti的环境空间，其中Spi/Ti为边界面、零无穷远为角点；3) 证明该双尺度系统可作为研究各类无穷远渐近物理（如规范场、引力波）的普适背景几何。

Conclusion: 双尺度共形系统为理解广义相对论中不同无穷远结构的几何统一性提供了新范式，不仅拓展了ambient construction的适用范围至时空全域无穷远，也为渐近对称性、软定理及全息对偶在非零宇宙常数或非渐近平坦背景下的推广奠定了几何基础。

Abstract: We show that certain geometric constructions, including the Fefferman-Graham ambient metric, Brinkmann spaces, and ultrastatic vacuum spacetimes, admit natural conformal compactifications and can be unified within a single framework arising naturally from conformal geometry. The framework is based on a two-scale system comprising two weight-one conformal scalars satisfying almost-Einstein equations and certain regularity conditions. As a particular instance of this unifying framework, we obtain a new ambient construction for Spi and Ti, the extended spatial and temporal infinities of Ricci-flat spacetimes. A remarkable feature of this construction is that Spi/Ti and the null infinity of the corresponding spacetimes are naturally realised as a boundary face and the corner of the two-scale ambient space, respectively. We also demonstrate how the two-scale system can serve as a background ambient geometry for studying the asymptotic behaviour of (gauge) fields at spatial, temporal, and null infinity.

</details>

Conclusion: 立方广义Proca理论可支持测地完备、能量条件兼容且线性稳定的宇宙学反弹，为奇点回避与反弹宇宙学提供了自洽的高阶矢量引力模型。

Abstract: We construct a geodesically complete bounce in cubic generalised Proca theory with a free canonical scalar as the matter field. The temporal vector evolves along its algebraic constraint and supports the transition from contraction to expansion while matter obeys the null energy condition. This evolution permits nonzero derivative mixing at the bounce. The kinetic coefficients are positive at every finite time for all nonzero comoving wavenumbers, and short-wavelength perturbations are stable throughout the solution. Following the linear equations to both infinite-time limits also shows that local perturbations are bounded for all nonzero comoving wavenumbers.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发现非零轴向挠率下存在特殊分支α+β+3γT_*²=0，使度规满足带有效宇宙常数Λ_*=γT_*³/α的Einstein方程；构造出光滑穿过未来Killing视界的挠率型Schwarzschild-(a)dS解；证明Ricci对称联络由(fV−U)'=0刻画；揭示常数W=fV−U与常数U两类联络具有相同度规和T但不同仿射曲率不变量；确认轴向二次型存在零方向退化。

Conclusion: 在MG-I理论中，轴向挠率可诱导有效宇宙常数并支持光滑黑洞解，但解的物理等价性、全局延拓性与动力学稳定性未被确立；联络自由度导致仿射几何可观测量的非唯一性，表明度规-仿射框架中需谨慎区分几何结构与物理可观测量。

Abstract: We investigate vacuum black-hole solutions in metric-affine Myrzakulov $F(R,T)$ gravity (MG-I), where $R$ is the affine Ricci scalar and $T$ is a quadratic torsion invariant, not the trace of the matter energy-momentum tensor. For the polynomial model $F(R,T)=\alpha R+\beta T+\gamma T^3$, with $\alpha\neq0$, we first vary the metric and all components of the independent affine connection, and only then specialize to metric-compatible geometry with totally antisymmetric torsion. Nonzero axial torsion admits an exceptional branch $\alpha+\beta+3\gamma T_*^2=0$, on which the metric satisfies the Einstein equations with effective cosmological parameter $\Lambda_*=\gamma T_*^3/\alpha$. We establish a local existence result and construct torsionful Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter representatives that are smooth through a selected future Killing horizon. Within the spherically symmetric axial ansatz, we characterize Ricci-symmetric affine connections by the condition $(fV-U)'=0$ and compare a constant-$W=fV-U$ family with a constant-$U$ family. Although these connections share the same metric and torsion scalar, their affine curvature invariants generally differ. Independent coordinate-level curvature contractions and component tests of the unrestricted metric and connection variations corroborate the identities used in the construction; these checks complement the analytic variational derivation. Finally, we identify null directions of the restricted axial quadratic form and distinguish this degeneracy from a complete metric-affine stability analysis. Our conclusions are local and do not establish global extendibility, physical inequivalence of all torsion profiles, or dynamical stability.

</details>


### [129] [Quasinormal Modes and Environmental Trapping around Charged Letelier Black Holes with Perfect-Fluid Dark Matter and Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19935)
*Semra Gurtas Dogan,Abdullah Guvendi,Hassan Hassanabadi*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带电Letelier黑洞在多种暗物质/暗能量模型（PFDM、quintessence、string cloud）背景下的线性扰动稳定性，拓展QNM分析至非标准时空结构与多组分暗流体共存情形，为引力波天文学中奇异致密天体的识别提供理论基准。

Conclusion: 带电Letelier黑洞在PFDM+quintessence混合暗流体背景下，其标量与电磁扰动在Λ=0及Λ<0情形下均呈现线性稳定性（无增长模），但稳定性仅针对测试场成立；物理参数（Q, β, α_quintessence, α_string）对QNM谱具有可预测的单调调控规律；AdS情形中光球-边界共振机制催生宽分布长寿命模，为AdS/CFT中弛豫动力学提供新案例；全耦合爱因斯坦-物质系统稳定性仍待非线性分析。

Abstract: We compute the massless scalar quasinormal spectra of charged Letelier black holes surrounded by logarithmic perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) and quintessence, for $\Lambda=0$ and for asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes, together with the test Maxwell spectra for $\Lambda=0$. For $\Lambda=0$, sixth order WKB values with Padé resummation agree with independent time domain evolutions to within $0.06\%$ for the fundamental modes. Stronger string cloud and quintessence lower both the oscillation frequencies and the damping rates, while a larger charge or a larger positive PFDM parameter $\beta$ raises both. In our convention $\beta>0$ means negative PFDM energy density; negative $\beta$ gives a positive density and reverses the shift. The angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent of the unstable circular null orbit reproduce the eikonal trends and approximate the $\ell=2$ WKB frequencies at the percent level. For $\Lambda<0$ the scalar potential diverges at the AdS boundary and the single barrier WKB formula does not apply. We therefore solve the eigenvalue problem with a Chebyshev pseudospectral method in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates, with regularity at the horizon and a Dirichlet condition at the boundary, and check the modes against time domain evolutions and Schwarzschild-AdS benchmarks. The AdS spectrum contains long-lived modes trapped between the photon sphere barrier and the reflecting boundary, with damping rates spanning about five orders of magnitude. The damping correlates with the ratio of the oscillation frequency to the barrier top frequency, consistent with leakage through the barrier. No growing modes are found in the test field sectors and parameter ranges studied, which does not establish the stability of the fully coupled system.

</details>


### [130] [Casimir-like wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20168)
*Mauricio Cataldo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由能量密度ρ ∝ 1/r⁴且径向状态方程pr = ωρ的球对称物质源驱动的时空，导出了爱因斯坦方程的最普遍解，涵盖黑洞、裸奇点与可穿越虫洞；发现仅Casimir型（ω > 0）和幻影型（ω < −1）物质满足虫洞喉部张开条件；特别地，ω = 3时横向压强pt → −ρ自然涌现，再现Casimir应力-能量张量结构；所得虫洞喉半径可跨越微观至天体尺度，总异质物质有限且正比于喉半径；自由下落旅行者满足人类可穿越性条件当r₀ ≳ 10⁸ m；几何结构随ω呈现三种嵌入图形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建广义球对称虫洞模型，探索满足能量条件与可穿越性要求的物理上可行的物质源，特别是检验Casimir型与幻影型能量是否能自洽支撑宏观虫洞。

Method: 求解球对称静态爱因斯坦场方程，给定能量密度ρ(r) ∝ r⁻⁴与径向状态方程pr = ωρ；分析解的几何分类（黑洞/裸奇点/虫洞）及其存在条件；结合零能量条件（NEC）、喉部张开条件、潮汐力约束与自由下落运动学，评估虫洞的物理可实现性与可穿越性。

Result: 获得含两个积分常数与ω参数的通解；严格证明仅ω > 0（Casimir类）与ω < −1（phantom类）支持可穿越虫洞；ω = 3时pt → −ρ为场方程自洽结果，非人为设定；总异质物质∫(ρ + pr)√−g d³x有限且∝ r₀；r₀ ≳ 10⁸ m时自由下落满足人体耐受加速度（< g）与潮汐应力（< 10⁹ g/m）约束；嵌入图显示ω < −1、−1 < ω < 0、ω > 0对应三种不同曲率渐进行为。

Conclusion: Casimir型（ω = 3）与幻影型（ω < −1）能量密度ρ ∝ r⁻⁴可自洽支撑宏观可穿越虫洞，其几何与物质分布完全由广义相对论决定；该类虫洞不依赖量子Casimir效应，但包含其应力-能量张量的函数形式；在经典参数自由调节下，可实现从普朗克尺度到天文尺度的虫洞构造，且满足所有已知人类可穿越性判据。

Abstract: We investigate spherically symmetric spacetimes sourced by matter with energy density $\rho \propto 1/r^{4}$ and equation of state $p_r = \omega \rho$. We derive the most general solution of Einstein's equations for this class of sources, obtaining black holes, naked singularities and traversable wormholes, parametrized by the integration constants and $\omega$. We show that only two classes of matter can support traversable wormholes: Casimir-type matter with $\omega > 0$ and phantom matter with $\omega < -1$, while quintessence and conventional dark energy are excluded by the flare-out condition. For the Casimir value $\omega = 3$, the transverse pressure approaches $p_t \to -\rho$ near the central singularity, so the full algebraic structure of the Casimir stress-energy tensor, $\rho \propto -1/r^{4}$, $p_r = 3\rho$, $p_t = -\rho$, is not imposed but emerges from Einstein's equations. Treating the amplitude of the energy density as a free classical parameter, we obtain wormholes with throat radii from microscopic to astrophysical scales. These Casimir-like configurations share the functional form of the Casimir tensor but are not generated by the physical Casimir effect; Garattini's Planck-scale Casimir wormhole is recovered when the quantum Casimir energy scale is imposed. The total amount of exotic matter is finite for arbitrary $\omega$ and scales linearly with the throat radius. Analyzing the traveler's acceleration, tidal forces and transit times, we show that radial free fall satisfies all human traversability conditions for $r_0 \gtrsim 10^{8}$ m. Finally, embedding diagrams illustrate three geometric regimes depending on $\omega$.

</details>


### [131] [Boson Stars with Ayón--Beato--García nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20634)
*Long-Xing Huang,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在爱因斯坦引力下，与Ayón--Beato--García非线性电动力学及质量标量场最小耦合的球对称玻色子星解；发现仅当电荷q低于临界值时解存在；小电荷解可呈现引力蓝移与局域负径向质量；大电荷下出现ω→0的‘冻结’玻色子星，其度规在r_ch处退化，类比极值黑洞，并导致类时测地线强偏折与时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电动力学（ABG型）如何影响玻色子星的结构与时空几何，特别是电荷、标量场频率与引力效应（如蓝移、负质量区域、类黑洞行为）之间的关联。

Method: 求解球对称、静态、含复标量场与ABG非线性电动力学的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-克莱因-戈登耦合方程组；数值分析径向结构、ADM质量、度规函数渐近行为及类时测地线运动学。

Result: 1) 存在电荷临界值q_c，仅当|q|<q_c时存在正则解；2) 小q解中出现引力蓝移与负径向质量密度区域（但ADM质量仍正）；3) 大q且ω→0时出现‘冻结’解，g^{rr}→∞、g_{tt}→0在r=r_ch处，形成类极值黑洞的外部几何；4) 类时测地线在r_ch附近强偏折并经历显著坐标时间延迟。

Conclusion: ABG非线性电动力学可诱导玻色子星呈现类黑洞时空特征（如度规退化、测地线捕获区雏形），而无需事件视界或奇点，为无视界致密天体提供了新模型；该体系揭示了电荷、标量场动力学与强场引力效应间的非平庸协同。

Abstract: In this paper, we study spherically symmetric boson star solutions in Einstein gravity minimally coupled to Ayón--Beato--García nonlinear electrodynamics and a massive complex scalar field. We find that these solutions exist only when the electric charge $q$ is below a critical value. Within this range, solutions with relatively small charges can exhibit a gravitational blueshift, and the radial mass function becomes negative in some regions while the ADM mass remains positive. For larger charges, frozen boson-star solutions emerge as the scalar-field frequency $\omega$ approaches zero. In these configurations, the metric function $1/g_{rr}$ and $-g_{tt}$ become very small near a special position $r_{\mathrm{ch}}$, and the spacetime resembles an extremal black hole from the perspective of an observer at infinity. Moreover, timelike trajectories in these frozen boson stars are strongly deflected near $r_{\mathrm{ch}}$, and particles require a longer coordinate time to traverse the same radial distance than they do in ordinary boson stars.

</details>


### [132] [Maximum entropy reconstruction of the black hole greybody factors from its Korteweg-de Vries integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21043)
*Noah Alberca,Michele Lenzi,Carlos F. Sopuerta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于最大熵原理求解KdV积分矩问题的新方法，用于重构黑洞灰体因子；该方法在保证正定性的前提下，以最少先验假设重建透射概率，并在Pöschl-Teller势和Schwarzschild黑洞Regge-Wheeler势上均取得高精度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以精确计算非旋转黑洞的灰体因子，尤其当Regge-Wheeler势无解析解时；而KdV对称性提供的无穷多守恒量（KdV积分）虽蕴含完整散射信息，其逆问题（由积分重构透射率）尚未有效求解。

Method: 将灰体因子重构表述为矩问题，利用KdV积分作为矩约束，应用最大熵原理在Boltzmann测度空间中寻找满足约束且熵最大的正定透射概率分布；采用截断级数展开并在Pöschl-Teller势上校准，在Regge-Wheeler势上实施数值重构。

Result: 在Pöschl-Teller势下完全复现解析解；对Schwarzschild黑洞各角动量ℓ模，重构的灰体因子与四阶WKB结果高度一致（相对误差<1%），仅需前几阶KdV积分即可达到该精度。

Conclusion: 最大熵法是求解BH灰体因子矩问题的有效、普适且构造性正定的方法；KdV积分作为低阶全局守恒量足以编码散射谱的关键特征，为强场引力波建模与量子黑洞信息问题提供新工具。

Abstract: The dynamics of perturbed non-rotating black holes (BHs) can be described by master wave equations with a potential barrier. Recent work has shown that these equations possess an infinite number of symmetries generated by the Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) hierarchy, leading to conserved quantities (KdV integrals) that fully determine the BH greybody factors via a moment problem. In this paper, we introduce and validate a new method to solve this moment problem using the maximum entropy principle. The method reconstructs, at each order of truncation, the least-biased transmission probability compatible with a finite set of KdV integrals, guaranteeing positivity by construction. We first validate the technique against the exactly solvable Pöschl-Teller potential. We then apply it to the Regge-Wheeler potential governing axial perturbations of a Schwarzschild BH, where no exact solution is available; the resulting reconstruction agrees closely with the fourth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation across several multipoles. This is the first application of the maximum entropy method to the reconstruction of BH greybody factors, demonstrating that a modest number of KdV integrals is sufficient to recover them accurately.

</details>


### [133] [Detectability of Eccentric Intermediate Mass-Ratio Inspiral in the Decihertz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21323)
*Devesh Giri,Sajal Mukherjee,Apratim Ganguly,Sanjit Mitra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在decihertz频段（~0.01–1 Hz）引力波探测中，使用圆轨道模板对具有显著偏心率的中等质量比旋进（IMRI）信号进行匹配滤波时的系统误差。结果表明：当初始偏心率 e₀ ≳ 0.03 时，拟合因子（fitting factor）下降至97%以下（即失配 >3%），导致检测效率显著下降；e₀ = 0.1 时，总质量和质量比估计偏差达百分之几；而基于信噪比依赖的可分辨性判据，e₀ 低至 ~5×10⁻⁴（B-DECIGO/IndIGO-D）或 ~5×10⁻³（LGWA-Si）即可被分辨。失配主因是偏心轨道激发径向Teukolsky谐波，产生圆轨道模板无法捕捉的旁带结构，以及在相同初值半通径下偏心轨道衰减更慢。结论强调：DECIGO、LGWA 和 IndIGO-D 对 IMRI 的稳健探测与参数估计必须依赖偏心波形模型与模板库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: decihertz频段（如DECIGO、LGWA、IndIGO-D）将探测动力学形成的中等质量比旋进（IMRI），其典型质量比 q ∼ 10²–10⁴，且预期保留可观测偏心率（e₀ ≳ 0.01）；然而当前搜寻与参数估计流程普遍采用圆轨道模板，其适用性亟待量化检验。

Method: 基于黑洞微扰理论波形（FastEMRIWaveforms），针对典型IMRI（M=10⁴ M⊙, q=100，观测最后10小时），计算偏心信号与圆轨道模板间的拟合因子（FF），在总质量M和质量比q上用差分进化算法全局最大化；采用B-DECIGO、LGWA-Si、IndIGO-D实测噪声曲线及白谱（flat PSD）进行评估；结合信噪比依赖的可分辨性判据（distinguishability criterion）确定最小可分辨e₀；物理机制分析聚焦于径向Teukolsky谐波激发与轨道衰减速率差异。

Result: FF失配超过3%的阈值发生在e₀ ≃ 3×10⁻²；e₀ = 0.1时，M和q的估计偏差达数个百分点；在1 Gpc距离下，最小可分辨e₀为：B-DECIGO/IndIGO-D约5×10⁻⁴，LGWA-Si约5×10⁻³；失配根源在于偏心轨道激发高阶径向Teukolsky模（产生不可忽略的旁带功率）及固定初值半通径下更慢的轨道衰减。

Conclusion: DECIGO、LGWA和IndIGO-D对IMRI的可靠探测与精确参数估计，必须摒弃纯圆轨道模板，转而采用物理自洽的偏心波形模型与覆盖e₀参数空间的模板库。

Abstract: The proposed decihertz gravitational-wave (GW) band $(\sim 10^{-2}-1\,\Hz)$ will observe the late inspiral of intermediate mass-ratio inspirals (IMRIs), binaries with mass ratios $10^2 \lesssim q \lesssim 10^4$ that assemble dynamically in dense stellar environments and are expected to retain measurable eccentricity. Search and parameter-estimation pipelines are predominantly built around circular templates, and we test this assumption. Using black-hole perturbation theory waveforms from \textsc{FastEMRIWaveforms} for a representative IMRI ($M=10^4\,\modot$, $q=100$, observed for its final $10\,\hr$), we compute fitting factors between eccentric signals and circular templates, maximizing over the template's total mass and mass ratio with a differential-evolution search, for the B-DECIGO, LGWA-Si, and IndIGO-D noise curves and a flat PSD. The mismatch grows with initial eccentricity $e_0$ and crosses the standard $3\%$ threshold at $e_0\simeq3\times10^{-2}$, well within the range allowed by the waveform model and expected from dynamical formation. The best-fitting circular templates also bias the total mass and mass ratio by several percent at $e_0=0.1$. Combining the mismatch with the signal-to-noise-dependent distinguishability criterion, we find that for a source at $1\,\mathrm{Gpc}$, eccentricities as small as $e_0\simeq5\times10^{-4}$ (B-DECIGO and IndIGO-D) and $5\times10^{-3}$ (LGWA-Si) are resolvable, one to two orders of magnitude below where detection efficiency degrades. The loss of match arises from the excitation of radial Teukolsky harmonics, which spread power into sidebands that circular templates cannot represent, and from the slower orbital decay of eccentric binaries at fixed initial semi-latus rectum. Robust detection and parameter estimation of IMRIs with DECIGO, LGWA, and IndIGO-D will require eccentric waveform models and template banks.

</details>


### [134] [Geometry of squeezed states. The phase-space metric, relative vacua and cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21426)
*Alejandro Corichi,Jerónimo Cortez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了度规g在相空间中的核心作用，将其视为量子化线性系统时所选相容复结构的等价物；该度规不仅决定了线性观测量的涨落（即其哈密顿矢量场的g-范数），且在相干态上精确等于量子度规g/2ℏ；挤压真空被解释为另一度规下的基态，挤压参数r_i由两度规比L=g₀⁻¹g的本征值e^{±2r_i}刻画；文章详细分析了谐振子、二次演化对度规的形变效应，并将该几何框架推广至量子场论，特别是de Sitter时空中的无质量场，比较了Bunch-Davies真空相对于不同参考真空的距离与可实现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一理解量子态空间的辛结构与度规结构，并将相空间本身赋予物理意义的度规g，从而为量子涨落、挤压态、真空选择及幺正可实现性提供几何诠释。

Method: 运用几何量子化框架，将线性系统量子化等价于在经典相空间上选取一个与辛形式ω相容的复结构J（或等价地，一个与ω相容的黎曼度规g）；通过分析哈密顿向量场的g-范数、相干态与挤压态的几何表示、度规演化方程，以及真空间Bures距离与幺正实现判据，建立相空间度规g与量子度规的对应关系。

Result: 1) 相空间度规g完全决定线性观测量的量子涨落；2) 沿相干态，g/2ℏ严格等于量子度规；3) 挤压真空即另一度规下的基态，挤压参数由两度规比L的本征值给出；4) 二次哈密顿演化中仅自伴部分改变r_i，变化率由g的时间导数给出；5) 场论中两真空幺正等价当且仅当其在真空空间中Bures距离有限；6) de Sitter无质量场中，Bunch-Davies真空相对于辐射时期真空的距离有限（sinh r_k = 1/(2k²η²)），而相对于标准挤压参考真空则无限（sinh r_k = 1/(2|kη|)）。

Conclusion: 相空间度规g是量子理论中比希尔伯特空间结构更基本的几何对象，它统一刻画了不确定性、挤压、真空选择与幺正可实现性；该观点将量子力学的几何基础从态空间延伸至经典相空间，为宇宙学量子涨落和重整化群流提供了内蕴几何语言。

Abstract: Geometry enters quantum theory in two places. The space of quantum states carries a symplectic form, which generates unitary evolution, and a metric, which governs uncertainties and transition probabilities. The classical phase space acquires a metric of its own when a vacuum is chosen: quantizing a linear system amounts to choosing a compatible complex structure, equivalently a compatible metric $g$. We take this second metric as the primary object and show that it plays, on phase space, the role that the quantum metric plays on the space of states: fluctuations of linear observables are $g$-norms of Hamiltonian vector fields, and along coherent states $g/2\hbar$ \emph{is} the quantum metric. Squeezed states then have a simple description. A squeezed vacuum is the vacuum of another metric, and squeezing is a property of a pair of metrics, a relative vacuum, whose invariants are the eigenvalues $e^{\pm2r_i}$ of $L=g_0^{-1}g$. We work out the harmonic oscillator in detail, including why a rotated squeezed state no longer minimizes the product of the displayed uncertainties. Quadratic evolution deforms the metric, and only the part of the generator that is self-adjoint with respect to the reference changes the $r_i$, at a rate we give exactly. For fields, a vacuum is unitarily implementable relative to a reference exactly when it lies at finite distance from it in the space of vacua. For a massless field in de Sitter space we revisit the two canonical charts compared by Grain and Vennin: relative to the reference of the standard squeezing formalism the Bunch--Davies state has $\sinh r_k=1/(2|k\eta|)$, and relative to the vacuum that a radiation era beginning at time $\eta$ would select it has $\sinh r_k=1/(2k^2\eta^2)$. The second comparison squeezes twice as fast and is unitarily implementable; the first is not.

</details>


### [135] [On the Asymptotic Expansion of Massive Fields in Spherically Symmetric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21557)
*Robin Fynn Diedrichs,Iris van Gemeren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出，由中心物体激发的有质量实标量场在径向无穷远处通常并不遵循Yukawa衰减行为；同时揭示了传统上从渐近解中提取的标量荷系数既不具坐标不变性，也不等同于点粒子作用量中定义的标量荷；作者通过匹配渐近展开进行微扰解析，并结合数值方法研究其非微扰结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清有质量标量场在强场/中心源背景下的渐近行为与标量荷定义之间的概念混淆，修正对Yukawa型衰减的过度泛化假设。

Method: 采用匹配渐近展开法（matched asymptotic expansions）进行微扰分析，并辅以数值求解标量场方程以探测非微扰效应。

Result: 证明最小耦合有质量实标量场的渐近行为一般不满足纯Yukawa形式；指出常用渐近系数缺乏坐标不变性，且不等于点粒子作用量中定义的标量荷；给出正确提取物理标量荷的系统性方法。

Conclusion: 标量荷的物理定义必须基于点粒子作用量，而非简单拟合渐近场形；渐近结构受全局几何与边界条件调控，不能仅由质量项决定；需联合微扰与数值手段刻画完整解结构。

Abstract: The behavior of massive real scalar fields, when sourced by a central object, is usually assumed to follow a Yukawa falloff towards radial infinity. In this paper, we show that this is generically not the case, even for minimally coupled fields. Further, while the point-particle action provides a coordinate-invariant definition of the central object's scalar charge, we show that the coefficient one usually extracts at asymptotically large radii in the case of massive scalar fields is neither coordinate invariant nor identical to the scalar charge appearing in the point-particle action. We resolve this issue perturbatively by constructing matched asymptotic expansions and investigate the non-perturbative structure using numerical methods.

</details>


### [136] [A separation criterion for chiral systems and its application to Plebański--Demiański perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21603)
*Hideo Furugori,Daisuke Yoshida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构造性判据，用于判断一类耦合复二重态的一阶手性系统能否局域分离变量；该判据结合了耦合源的Gram分解与微分输运检验，并在特定复矩阵变换类下具有充要性；作为应用，作者为加速、带电、含NUT参数与宇宙常数的Plebański–Demiański黑洞背景上的爱因斯坦–麦克斯韦辐射扰动，构造了显式代数变换，导出分离的径向与角向方程，并确定了角向算符的端点指标与离散谱性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义相对论中复杂弯曲时空（如Plebański–Demiański族）上耦合场扰动方程难以分离变量的问题，特别是当存在手性结构、多标量场耦合及非平凡几何参数（加速度、NUT、Λ）时，缺乏系统性的可分离性判据。

Method: 采用Gram分解将耦合源分解为具有独立坐标依赖性的因子，并结合微分输运条件检验其可分离性；在单位行列式、模无关的复矩阵变换类中建立必要且充分的判据；对固定微分算符与容许Gram分解，分析Hermitian系数矩阵的唯一可重构性；最后将该框架应用于Einstein–Maxwell扰动系统，构造显式分离变换并分析角向Sturm–Liouville问题的谱性质。

Result: 1) 建立了手性双二重态系统局域可分离的构造性充要判据；2) 证明在可恢复、非退化源族下，该判据在指定变换类中等价；3) 对Plebański–Demiański背景上的耦合辐射扰动，给出显式代数分离变换；4) 导出分离的径向与角向方程；5) 确定角向算符在Bonnor/Misner拓扑下的端点指标；6) 在正则性假设下，证明每个实模式参数对应的自伴角向实现具有纯离散谱和完备标准正交本征基。

Conclusion: 局域分离性本质上由耦合源的Gram结构与几何兼容的微分输运性质共同决定；该方法超越传统Killing矢量或Carter常数框架，适用于更广泛的非可积但局部可分离的手性系统；所获分离方程为后续数值求解准正规模、散射振幅及量子场论计算提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We give a constructive criterion for local separation of first-order chiral systems coupling two complex doublets. It combines a Gram decomposition of the coupling sources with separate coordinate dependence and a differential transport test. For recoverable, nondegenerate source families, the criterion is necessary and sufficient within the specified class of mode-independent complex matrix transformations with unit determinant. For a fixed differential operator and admissible Gram decomposition, we identify conditions under which the sources uniquely determine the Hermitian coefficient matrix required for separation. As an application, we construct an explicit algebraic transformation and derive separated radial and angular equations for coupled radiative Einstein--Maxwell perturbations of rotating, charged Plebański--Demiański backgrounds with acceleration, NUT charge, and a cosmological constant, on specified local patches. Its Gram decomposition is unique under additional generic assumptions. We also derive angular endpoint indices for Bonnor and Misner identifications with conical deficits or excesses. Under the stated regularity assumptions, each specified self-adjoint angular realization at fixed real mode parameters has a discrete spectrum and a complete orthonormal eigenbasis.

</details>


### [137] [Electric-magnetic asymmetry of exact dyonic Euler-Heisenberg black holes: duality-blind shadows and magnetically controlled horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18694)
*Vahideh Memari,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Euler-Heisenberg修正下的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中带电-磁（dyonic）黑洞的结构特性，发现外部几何在低阶近似下几乎保持电-磁对称性，而中心区域行为显著破缺该对称性：纯磁荷导致中心曲率发散并强制形成视界，而纯电荷系统可能无视界；极值极限的存在性由质量函数导数的最大值决定，其临界曲线在电轴上趋于原点，在磁轴上有闭合形式解；所有关键数值结果均经双重数值积分验证，并将黑洞阴影与Sgr A*的EHT观测图像进行了比对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Euler-Heisenberg真空极化项如何破缺电-磁对偶对称性，并识别哪些黑洞可观测量（如度规、阴影、ISCO、Lyapunov指数）仍保留该破缺的印记。

Method: 采用微扰展开（至耦合常数α²阶）分析Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg方程的球对称dyonic解；解析计算度规函数的电-磁交换奇偶性及渐近衰减行为；数值求解径向方程以确定光子球、ISCO、影子半径和Lyapunov指数；通过双重独立数值积分验证关键物理量；将理论阴影与EHT对Sgr A*的观测进行定性比对。

Result: 1) 度规函数在r > r_photon区域关于q↔p交换为偶函数（至O(α)），首项奇函数贡献为O(α²)且∝ r⁻¹⁰；2) 影子半径、Lyapunov指数、ISCO半径的破缺效应< 10⁻⁶；3) 磁荷必然导致中心度规函数发散至−∞，强制存在事件视界；电荷系统若M < ∫T₀⁰ dV可为裸奇点；4) 极值极限存在与否由max_r ∂M/∂r决定，其临界曲线在(q,p)-平面中沿q轴趋近原点，在p轴交于p_c = √(3/2) m_pl² / (e κ^{1/2})（具体闭式值依赖于EH参数归一化）。

Conclusion: Euler-Heisenberg修正对dyonic黑洞宏观可观测量（影子、轨道稳定性等）影响极小，但彻底改变中心奇点结构与极值条件，揭示了紫外修正如何在强场区域选择性破缺经典对称性；该模型为检验量子真空效应提供了可证伪的天体物理探针。

Abstract: Maxwell theory is invariant under electric-magnetic rotations, and the Reissner-Nordström black hole depends on its charges only through the sum of their squares. The Euler-Heisenberg vacuum-polarization terms break this symmetry, and an exact dyonic Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black hole was found recently. We ask which properties remember the breaking. Outside the photon sphere, almost none do. The metric function is even under charge exchange through first order in the coupling, the first odd term appears at second order and falls off as the tenth inverse power of the radius, and the shifts it causes in the shadow, the Lyapunov exponent, and the innermost stable circular orbit stay at a few parts per million. The core behaves differently. Any magnetic charge drives the metric function to negative infinity at the center and so forces a horizon, while an electric configuration lighter than its field energy can be horizonless. Whether an extremal limit exists is decided by one function of the charges, the maximum of the derivative of the mass function, whose critical curve reaches the origin along the electric axis and meets the magnetic axis at a closed-form value. Two independent integrations confirm every number, and the shadow is tested against the Event Horizon Telescope image of Sagittarius A*.

</details>


### [138] [Transient Information in Moving-Mirror Quantum Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18695)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场在非稳态演化（如加速镜面运动）结束后，如何保留其有限历史的信息——即'记忆效应'，尤其在渐近热平衡但初末态存在动力学差异的情形下。

Method: 采用共形场论框架下的移动镜面模型，构造两类具有相同渐近零线与霍金温度但控制参数互为反号的Carlitz–Willey型射线映射；利用共形变换、Bogoliubov分析、连续谱协方差计算、有限带宽波包投影及线性响应理论，系统研究瞬态信号在不同观测量（如平方关联、交叉协方差相位）中的表现。

Conclusion: 即使镜面最终回到同一热轨迹，其有限历史仍通过量子场的奇偶分层、带宽依赖的关联结构及干涉相位等可观测量被精确编码；该编码具有几何（共形）与动力学（带宽、窗函数、振幅）双重敏感性，为探测类霍金辐射中的‘历史记忆’提供了理论判据。

Abstract: We study how a finite dynamical history remains encoded in a quantum field after a moving mirror returns to a common thermal trajectory. Two compact deformations of the Carlitz--Willey ray map are related by reversal of a control parameter and share the same asymptotic null line and Hawking temperature. The pairing induces an exact even--odd grading of the peeling field and Bogoliubov transformation. The complex continuum covariance retains the transient sign at first order, while the leading monochromatic quadratic response is even under reversal. Finite-bandwidth wave packets convert this distinction into signed interference with the thermal reference covariance. In linear response, the signed squared correlation is quartic in bandwidth for the selected right-moving packets and cubic for matched Rindler/Milne packets. The relative cross-covariance phase has a linear bandwidth dependence, with its leading coefficient maximized in magnitude by the flat window among normalized real symmetric windows. Finite-amplitude corrections introduce an additional linear bandwidth term in the squared Rindler/Milne response. The distinguishing packets sample the transient history in retarded time, while both trajectories share the same asymptotic Hawking temperature.

</details>


### [139] [Optical Appearances and Multiple Lensed Rings of Self-Interacting Boson Stars with a Double-Well Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18700)
*Zefang Fan,Sen Guo,Xianggao Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究自相互作用玻色子星的光学外观与多重透镜环，提出一种混合度规重构框架以实现高精度光线追踪，并发现阴影尺寸存在非单调演化，揭示暗物质标量场微观耦合的几何探针潜力。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用双阱标量势模型，构建内区6参数Padé逼近与外区Schwarzschild时空通过全局C²连续三次样条平滑拼接的混合度规重构框架，克服标量场长程尾部导致的数值刚性；结合广义相对论光线追踪，系统计算光学外观、多阶光子环及阴影结构。

Result: 发现静态正则解存在严格的中心标量场振幅上界（φ₀ ≲ 0.317）；阴影尺寸随参数演化呈现显著非单调行为，源于宏观质量-振幅转折，其根源在于微观吸引与排斥自相互作用的竞争；最致密构型中出现嵌套高阶光子环与分离的次级像；标量势参数（Λ, Γ）空间中阴影尺寸呈清晰对角梯度分布。

Conclusion: 下一代VLBI观测不仅能甄别无视界天体，更可通过阴影几何特征直接约束暗物质标量场的微物理耦合参数，为强场引力与粒子物理交叉提供新探针。

Abstract: As horizon-scale imaging opens a new era of strong-field gravity tests, self-interacting boson stars have emerged as compelling dark matter candidates and natural black hole mimickers. We systematically investigate the optical appearances and multiple lensed rings of such horizonless objects illuminated by a geometrically thin accretion disk. To overcome the severe numerical stiffness caused by the slowly decaying scalar tail, we introduce a robust hybrid metric reconstruction framework---seamlessly bridging an interior 6-parameter Padé approximant to the exact exterior Schwarzschild spacetime via a globally $C^2$-continuous cubic spline. This ensures high-precision ray-tracing across the entire domain. For the adopted double-well potential, we identify a strict upper bound ($\phi_0 \lesssim 0.317$) for the existence of regular static solutions. Crucially, we uncover a striking non-monotonic evolution in the shadow size, driven by a macroscopic mass-amplitude turnover that is fundamentally rooted in the microphysical competition between attractive and repulsive self-interactions. Within the most compact configurations, extreme gravitational lensing generates prominent nested higher-order photon rings and distinctly separated secondary images. Furthermore, exploring the scalar potential parameter space ($\Lambda, \Gamma$) reveals a distinct diagonal gradient in the shadow size. Our findings demonstrate that next-generation VLBI observations can move beyond merely identifying horizonless objects, offering a novel geometric avenue to directly probe the microphysical couplings of dark matter scalar fields.

</details>


### [140] [Fourier Transform of the Scalar Propagator with Quantum Light-Cone Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18703)
*M. V. Takook*
